tolkāppiya urittoki
tolkāppiyam nam vāḻviyal āvaṇam; nam muntiyar kaṇṭa moḻiyiyal vaḷaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum tiraṭṭit tanta tēṉ tēṭṭu!
attēṭṭic cuvitta kōttumpikaḷ paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ. avarkaḷ urikaḷiyellām oṉṟu tiraṭṭi, variciyuṟat tamiḻulakam koḷḷa vitta urittokutikaḷ iviyām!
muṉṉip patippāciriyarkaḷ teḷivuṟuttum maṇikkuvikaḷ ellāmum, avarkaḷ vitta vippuppaṭi vitta ceppēṭukaḷ it tolkāppiya urit tokiyām. mēlum, ivi kiṭittaṟku ariya karuvūlamumām!
***** ***** *****
iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, pērāciriyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar,  teyvac ciliyār, kallāṭar ākiya paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ, uri viḷakkam pala kālaṅkaḷil eḻutiyuḷar. eḻuttu, col, poruḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ patippukaḷ orē kālattil oruvarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭa villi. avaṟṟiyellām ōriṭattil orē vēḷiyil oṭṭu mottamāka muṉṉip patippāciriyarkaḷ uri - viḷakkam - kuṟippu - iṇippu ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ peṟa veḷiyiṭum ariya periya muyaṟciyil veḷiyiṭappaṭuvatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ ippatippākum. tolkāppiyattiṟkuk kiṭitta urikaḷ aṉittiyum orumottamākat taruvatāl itu, tolkāppiya urittoki eṉappaṭṭatu. tokiyāvatu tokukkappaṭṭatu. 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



aṟimukavuri
va.u. citamparaṉār
vaḷḷi nāyaka - ulakanāta - citamparaṉār,  `va.u.ci. eṉa mūṉṟeḻuttil muttamiḻp pukaḻum koṇṭavar avar!
nāṭum moḻiyum  namimru kaṇkaḷ
eṉṟu koṇṭa perumakkaḷi viral viṭṭu maṭakkaka eṇṇiṉ - avaṟṟukkē tammi īkam ceytavari eṇṇiṉ - aṭutta viralila maṭakka - āḻamāka eṇṇit tāṉē āka vēṇṭum!
ōṭṭappiṭārattil, ulakanātar - paramāyiyar makaṉārāka 05.09.1892 il piṟantavar va.u.ci. tantiyār vaḻakkaṟiñar:
tāmpiṟanta ūri irunta `vīrap perumāḷ aṇṇāvi eṉpāriṭam tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaraḷik kaṟṟār. tūttukkuṭi kiṟittava uyarpaḷḷi, tirunelvēli maturi tiraviyam tāyumāṉavar intuk kallūri uyarpaḷḷi āyavaṟṟil kaṟṟār.
tantiyār kālṭuvel kallūriyil cērttup payilac ceytum, kalvi nāṭṭam illārāy ūrkku vantu vaṭṭa āṭciyar aluvalakattil eḻuttar paṇi ceytār. atuvum ēṟkāmal caṭṭam paṭikkat tiruccirāppaḷḷi vantu caṭṭak kallūriyil payiṉṟu ki.pi. 1895il vaḻakkaṟiñarāṉār. paṭikkum pōtē 1894il tirumaṇamum āyatu. maṇamakaṉār vaḷḷiyammi. vaṟiyavarkku vātāṭalum, kāvalarit tiṇaṟavikka viṉāvalum koṇṭa va.u.ci.yiṉmēl, araciṉpaki maḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. tantiyār tūttukkuṭikkuc cella vittār.
vaḷḷiyammi 1900il iyaṟki eyta, avar uṟaviṉarāya mīṉāṭciyammiyi maṇakkavum nērntatu.
citamparaṉār tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟāl eṉṉa? avar īkamum tuṇivum avaruṭaṉ tāṉē irukkum! tūttukkuṭi perunakar! toṇṭukkum vāyppu! ekiptu koli vaḻakku! viṭuviṭuppu muṭipu! ēḻimiyar tōḻami yarāṉār!
tamiḻil toyvum, civa camaya īṭupāṭum perukap peruvāyppu ēṟpaṭa `vivēkapāṉu eṉṉum itaḻ naṭattiṉār. itu mātiki itaḻ.
ceṉṉikkuc ceṉṟu tirumpum oru cūḻal ēṟpaṭṭatu. appoḻutu citamparkku ōr eḻucci uṇṭāyatu; vaṇikattāl nērnta ayalārāṭciyi oḻikka, avvaṇikamē vaḻi eṉṟu tiṭṭam tōṉṟik kappal iyakkutalil muṉintātar.
mumpi ceṉṟu kappal vāṅki, tūttukkuṭikkum keḻumpukkum iṭiyē celuttiṉār. `cutēcik kappal āṅkilarik kotikka vikkātā? cutēci paṇṭacāli, neytal cāli, eṉpavaṟṟi niṟuviṉār. ālip pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷil muṉṉiṉṟār. tilakarit talivarākak koṇṭār. cūrattil nikaḻnta mānāṭṭil paṅkukoṇṭu (1907) mīṇṭu mēṭikaḷil araciyal muḻakkamiṭṭār. cuppiramaṇiyē civā uṭaṉāṉār! māvaṭṭa āṭciyar viñcu eṉpār citamparaṉār, civā iruvar mītum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pala cāṟṟi, nāṭukaṭattavum tiṭṭamiṭṭār.
muṟimaṉṟam 20 āṇṭu, kaṭuṅkāval taṇṭam vitikka, ataṉi `iṟivaṉ aruḷ eṉṟā. mēl muṟiyīṭukaḷāl, āṟāṇṭukaḷ ākik kaṇalpoṟi iyakki, cekkiḻuttu 1912il viṭutali peṟṟār.
ciṟiyil cēmcu ālaṉ nūlkaḷi moḻi peyarttār. viṭutalikkuppiṉ tamiḻt toṇṭil āḻamāka iṟaṅkiṉār. tolkāppiya iḷampūraṇar uriyi 1936il patippittār. tirukkuṟaḷ aṟattuppāl virivuri 1935il veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. avartam āyvum tuṇivum tirukkuṟaḷ uriyil veḷippaṭṭatu. avar eṇṇiyavāṟu muḻuturi kaṇṭum muḻutuṟa veḷippaṭavilli!
ciṟiyil avar eḻutiya `cuyacaritiyiṉ arumi paṭippār neñci urukkum! accariti 1946il pāri niliyattiṉarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭup pala patippukaḷ kaṇṭatu.
tirukkuṟaḷ aṟattuppāl uri, ciṅkappūr tamiḻttiru. kōvalaṅ kaṇṇaṉārāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭu kuṟaḷ kūṟiya ovvoruvarukkum ilavayamāka vaḻaṅkiyatu. va.u.ci. yiṉ vaṇmik katireḷi eṉat tōṉṟiyatu. 14ām māṭiyil veḷiyiṭṭu avvuri nayam eḷiyēṉ kūṟa, avvurip perumi aviyit tiḷikkac ceytatu!
kapparēlāṭṭiya tamiḻar - tamiḻk kappalum ōṭṭiya
tōṉṟal - tam nilikuṟittu oruveṇpāvil tāmē
ōṭukiṟār; ōṭṭappiṭārattār allarō!
vantakavi ñarkkellām māriyeṉap palporuḷum
tanta citamparaṉ tāḻntiṉṟu- cantamiḻ veṇ
pāc collip piccikkup pārellām ōṭukiṉṟāṉ
nāc collum tōlum nalintu
ē! tāḻnta tamiḻakamē! eṉṟatu meytāṉē!
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.



tolkāppiyam ariya patippukaḷum tēvikaḷum
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭip pakutiyil  tamiḻiṉ paṇṭi ilakkaṇa/ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ acciṭap peṟṟaṉa. avvakiyil tamiḻil accaṭikkappaṭṭa mutal ilakkaṇa nūlāka iṉṟu aṟiyappaṭuvatu naṉṉūlē.    kāraṇam tamiḻaka maṭaṅkaḷ naṉṉūlip payiṟṟilakkaṇamākavum payaṉ pāṭṭu ilakkaṇamākavum koṇṭiruntaṉa.  1835 -il tiruttaṇiki vicākap perumāḷiyar tāmē uriyeḻuti naṉṉūl mūlamum kāṇṭikiyuriyum eṉṉum talippil veḷiyiṭṭār. toṭarntu tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyārum,
a. muttuccāmi piḷḷiyum iṇintu ilakkaṇap pañcakam eṉṉum talippil naṉṉūl,  nampiyakap poruḷ, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlaṅkaḷi veḷik koṇarn taṉar. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka 1838 -il vīramā muṉivariṉ toṉṉūl viḷakkam accākkam peṟṟatu. 1847 -il tāṉ maḻavi makāliṅkiyarāl tamiḻiṉ tol ilakkaṇam tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāram uriyōṭu acciṭappaṭṭatu. 
toṭarntu yāḻppāṇattu nallūr āṟumukanāvalar, yāḻppāṇattu ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kōmaḷapuram irācakōpālapiḷḷi, cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyār, pōṉṟōr 19 -ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, tolkāppiyattik kiṭitta urikaḷōṭu patippittaṉar. 1935 - il tolkāppiyat tiṟku eḻutappaṭṭa urikaḷ  (iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷa aṉittu urikaḷum) patippikkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. 1847 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri eṭṭup patippukaḷum, 1885-2003 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar muṉṉintiyal eṭṭup patippukaḷum, ceyyuḷiyal nacciṉārkkiṉiyar taṉittu mūṉṟu patippukaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 1892 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri aintu patippukaḷum, 1868 - 2006 vari eḻuttu iḷampūraṇam ēḻu patippukaḷum, 1920 - 2005 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇam pattup patippukaḷum, 1927 - 2005 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram iḷampūraṇam āṟu patippukaḷum, 1885 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram pērāciriyam ēḻu patippukaḷum, 1929 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram teyvacciliyār mūṉṟu patippukaḷum, 1964 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram kallāṭam mūṉṟu patippukaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa eṉa muṉivar pā. matukēvaraṉ tamatu tolkāppiyam patippu āvaṇam nūlil  kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
2003 -il tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vāyilākat tolkāppiya urikaḷ (iḷampūraṇam, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, pērāciriyam, cēṉāvariyam, kallāṭam, teyvacciliyam) paṇṭita vitvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar, muṉivar na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu, mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ vāḻviyal viḷakkattuṭaṉ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 
iruppiṉum accil iṉṟu kiṭikkāta  ariya patippukaḷi mīḷ patippākap patippikka vēṇṭum eṉṉum nōkkattil, ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippu, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār patippu, va.u. citamparam piḷḷi patippu, irā. veṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi patippu, ci. kaṇēciyar patippu,  te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār patippu  ākiyōriṉ patippukaḷuṭaṉ tolkāppiyam toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷum iṇittu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vaḻi mīḷ patippukaḷākap patippikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mīḷ patippil piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷa potu neṟikaḷ
 mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ tolkāppiyam kuṟitta viḷakkam, ovvōr atikāram paṟṟiya vāḻviyal viḷakkam, uriyāciriyar vivaram ākiyavi nūliṉ toṭakkat til tokuttu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippukaḷiṉ mukappup pakkaṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippāciriyar paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kāla aṭippaṭiyil tokuttaḷikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ovvōr iyaliṉ  toṭakkattil,   pērāciriyar ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār tolkāppiyam tokuppil eḻuti yuḷḷa tolkāppiya iyal paṟṟiya viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippil  aṭikkuṟippukaḷ anta antap pakkattil tarappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil ovvōr iyalukkum toṭar  ilakkamiṭṭu, antanta iyaliṉ iṟutiyil tokuttu  vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
	muṉṉip patippukaḷil nūṟpākkaḷik kiyāṇṭuḷḷa muṟiyiyē ip patippilum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nūṟpāk kaḷukkut tamiḻ eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi  naṭi muṟiyil ippōtu uḷḷa eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūṟpā akaramutali, mēṟkōḷ akaramutali pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkup pakka eṇkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi nūṟpā eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
piṉṉiṇippākat tolkāppiyam toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa  kaṭṭurikaḷ iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūṟpā mutaṟ kuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷa patippukaḷil avaṟṟōṭu, 2003 tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vaḻiyē paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar muṉivar 
na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarkaḷākak koṇṭu veḷivanta ilakkaṇap patippil iṇikkap paṭṭirunta, 
-	nūṟpā niral
-	mēṟkōḷ col niral
-	mēṟkōḷ coṟṟoṭar niral
-	ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷ niral
-	kalic col niral( nūṟpāvaḻi)
-	kaliccol  niral (urivaḻi)
ākiyavi eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷ cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	muṉṉip patippil vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi appaṭiyē kiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	tolkāppiyap patippukaḷ kuṟitta vivaram tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippu  - 1892
collatikāram -  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 	
1868 - il tolkāppiyam collatikāram cēṉāvari yaruriyip patippitta ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, 1892 ām āṇṭu collatikāram nacciṉārkiṉiyar uriyip patippittār. collatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri toṭarcciyākap patippikkappaṭṭālum, mutalil patippitta ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷiyiṉ patippu iṉṟu accuvaṭivil puḻakkattil illi. appatippiṉi veḷiyiṭum nōkkattil ippatippu mīḷ patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. ippatippil nūṟpā mutaṟkuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntatu. attuṭaṉ maṟṟa iṇippukaḷum iṇikkappaṭṭu, mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ avarkaḷiṉ vāḻviyal viḷakkamum, ovvōr iyaliṉ toṭakkattil ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa iyal viḷakkamum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attuṭaṉ collatikāram toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷ piṉṉiṇippāka iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār patippu - 1927
collatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
	 iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram poruḷatikāram urikaḷ muṉpē veḷivantaṉa. collatikāram   iḷampūraṇar uri 1927 - il kāvērippākkam namaccivāya mutaliyāriṉ ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṉi si. kumāracāmi nāyuṭu saṉsāl  veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. ituvē collatikāram iḷampūraṇar uriyiṉ mutaṟ patippākum. ip patippil aṭikkuṟippukaḷ antantap pakkattil koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil toṭar  ilakkamiṭṭu, ovvōr iyaliṉ muṭivil  aṭik kuṟippukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ip patippil   nūṟpākkaḷukkut tamiḻ eṇvariciyum naṭimuṟi eṇ variciyum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil naṭimuṟi eṇ muṟi maṭṭum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
va.u. citamparampiḷḷi patippu - 1928 , 1931, 1933, 1935
eḻuttatikāram, poruḷatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
1868 il tiricirapuram cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyārip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu kaṉṉiyappa mutaliyārāl patippikkappaṭṭatu.  itaṉi aṭuttu 1928 -il tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram patavuri eṉṉum peyaril va.u. citamparam piḷḷi patippu vēlāyutam piriṇṭiṅ pira, tūttukkuṭiyiliruntu veḷivantatu. appatippiṉ toṭakkattil koṭukkappaṭṭirunta  nūṟpā akarāti ippatippiṉ nūliṉ iṟutiyil koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. attuṭaṉ col niral (mēṟkōḷ), coṟṟoṭar niral (mēṟkōḷ), ceyyuḷ niral (mēṟkōḷ) kalic col niral (nūṟpā vaḻi) kalic col niral (urivaḻi) tokuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1920 -il kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇam uri mutal pakutiyākak kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal mūṉṟu iyalkaḷiyum si. kumāracāmi nāyuṭu saṉ niṟuvaṉattār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. maṟṟa pakutikaḷ veḷivantatāka aṟiya iyalavilli. ippatippiṟkup piṉ va.u. citamparam piḷḷi tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam poruḷatikāram akattiṇiyiyal, puṟattiṇiyiyal ākiya iru iyalkaḷ iṇinta patippu, ceṉṉip pirampūril iruntu veḷivantatu. ippatippil āṇṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli. āṉāl 1921- il veḷivanta kuṟippiṉi, tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam eḻuttikāram patavuri patippil kuṟippiṭukiṟār. kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal 1933 - il vāviḷḷa irāmavāmi cāṭrulu aṇṭ saṉ mūlam veḷi vantuḷḷatu. 1935 - il mēṟkaṇṭa patippāḷarkaḷik koṇṭu meyppāṭṭiyal, uvamiyiyal, ceyyuḷiyal, marapiyal patippittuḷḷār. mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka veḷivantālum  avaṟṟiṟkum toṭar eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  1935 - il oruṅkiṇinta patippāka va.u. citamparam piḷḷi 
e. viyāpurippiḷḷi ākiyōr patippāciriyarkaḷāka amintu veḷivantuḷḷatu.  taṉittaṉip patippukaḷāka mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka  veḷivantuḷḷa patippiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu im mīḷ patippu patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
irā. veṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi patippu  - 1929
collatikāram  - teyvac ciliyār
 1929 -il karantit tamiḻc caṅkattāl mutaṉ mutalil patippikkappaṭṭatu. patippāciriyar rā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi. itaṉ niḻaṟpaṭivam 1984 -il tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.  ip patippil cūttira mutaṟ kuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntatu. attuṭaṉ col niral (mēṟkōḷ), coṟṟoṭar niral (mēṟkōḷ), ceyyuḷ niral (mēṟkōḷ) kalic col niral (nūṟpā vaḻi) kalic col niral ( urivaḻi) tokuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ci. kaṇēciyar patippu - 1937, 1938, 1943,1948
eḻuttu nacciṉārkkiṉārkiṉiyar, collatikāram  cēṉāvariyar poruḷ -  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, pērāciriyar
1847 - il maḻavi makāliṅkiyarāl eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyam patippikkappaṭṭatu. 1891 - il ci. vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippum 1923 - il 
ta. kaṉakacuntarattiṉ patippum kaḻakattiṉ mūlamum veḷivantaṉa. 1937 - il nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl kaṇēciyar eḻutiya uriviḷakkak kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ eḻuttatikāram mūlamum nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruriyum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
1868  -il yāḻppāṇam nallūr āṟumuka nāvalar patippu, ceṉṉapaṭṭaṇam  ū. puṣparatac ceṭṭiyāratu kalāratnākara accukkūṭattil ci.vi. tāmōratam piḷḷiyāl patippikkappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭu kōmaḷapuram irācakōpāla piḷḷiyiṉ patippum tolkāppiyam cēṉāvariyam eṉṟa peyaril veḷivantuḷḷatu. 1923 - il kantacāmiyār tiruttiya tiruttaṅkaḷōṭum kuṟippuriyōṭum kaḻakap patippāka veḷivantatu. 1938 -il ci. kaṇēciyar patippu avar eḻutiya uriviḷakkak kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
1885 -il ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷiyāl tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iyaṟṟiya uriyōṭum palatēcap piratirūpaṅkaḷik koṇṭu paricōtittu yāḻppāṇam ci.vi. tāmōtararāl patippikkappaṭṭatu eṉṉum kuṟippuṭaṉ patippu veḷivantatu. appatippil uḷḷa piṉṉāṉkiyal nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri aṉṟu eṉṟu maṟuttu irā. irākaviyaṅkār 1902 - 1905 variyāṉa centamiḻ itaḻil āyvukkaṭṭuri eḻutit teḷivupaṭuttiṉār.  ataṉit toṭarntu poruḷatikāram muḻutum nāṉku pakutiyāka nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, pērāciriyam eṉap pirittu 1916, 1917 - ām āṇṭukaḷil ca. pavaṉāntam piḷḷi patippittār. 1917 - il rā. irākaviyaṅkār ceyyuḷiyalukku nacciṉārkkiyar uri iruppati aṟintu, nacciṉārkiṉiyar uri uriyāciriyar uriyuṭaṉ eṉa iru urikaḷiyum iṇittu veḷiyiṭṭār. 1934, 1935 -il e. viyāpurippiḷḷi avarkaḷāl ōlip pirati paricōtikkappaṭṭu mā.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi avarkaḷatu ariya ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉum tiruttaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram mutaṟpākam veḷivantatu. 1948 - il tolkāppiya muṉivarāl iyaṟṟap paṭṭa tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram (mutaṟpākam) muṉṉintiyalum nacciṉārkkiṉiyamum eṉa, ci. kaṇēciyar eḻutiya ārāyccik kuṟippuriyuṭaṉ nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl patippikkappaṭṭatu. 1943 -il tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram (iraṇṭām pākam) eṉpatōṭu piṉṉāṉkiyalum pērāciriyamum ivi puṉṉālik kaṭṭuvaṉ tamiḻ vittuvāṉ, piramarī ci. kaṇēciyar avarkaḷ ēṭṭup piratikaḷōṭu oppunōkkit tiruttiya tiruttaṅkaḷōṭum eḻutiya uri viḷakkak kuṟippukaḷōṭum īḻakēcari atipar nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl tamatu cuṉṉākam, tirumakaḷ aḻuttakattil patippikkapaṭṭaṉa eṉa variyaṟukkap paṭṭiruntatu.
2007-il ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉam maṟupatippāka ci. kaṇēciyariṉ patippip patittuḷḷatu. appatippiṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē ippatippu uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  cūttira akarāti, cūttira arumpata viḷakkam ākiyavaṟṟiṟku ippatippil pakka eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avi im mīḷpatippil nūṟpā eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṉupantamāka 
ci. kaṇēciyyar cila kaṭṭurikaḷi appatippil iṇit tiruntār avaṟṟōṭu mēlum avaruṭiya cila kaṭṭurikaḷ ippatippil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār patippu  1971
collatikāram kallāṭam 
ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi tolkāppiyam poruḷati kāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri patippuriyil (1885) kallāṭar uri paṟṟiya kuṟippiṉit tarukiṟār.  ṭi.eṉ appaṉiyaṅkār centamiḻ itaḻil (1920, tokuti-19, pakuti-1, pakkam-20) kallāṭaruri eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyil rīmāṉ em cēkṣakiri cātiriyār (1893) kallāṭar uri kuṟittu ārāyntuḷḷatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār. cōḻavantāṉ aracañcaṇmukaṉār (1905) rā. irākaviyaṅkār (1917) peyar viḻiyāṉ, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār(1920) navanītakiruṣṇa pārati(1920) pi. citampara puṉṉivaṉanāta mutaliyār(1922) kantacāmiyār (1923) va.u. cimparam piḷḷi (1928) maṉṉārkuṭi tamiḻp paṇṭitar ma.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi (1929) araṅka. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi (1929) ākiyōr kallāṭar uri kuṟitta kuṟippukaḷit tamatu kaṭṭurikaḷil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.  piṉṉaṅkuṭi cā. cuppiramaṇiya cātiriyār kallāṭar uri ōriyaṇṭal kiyeḻuttup puttaka cāliyil uḷḷatu eṉṟum, avvuri evviyal vari uḷḷatu eṉṉum kuṟippuriyum tarukiṟār.
1950 - 1952  vari tarumapura ātīṉam veḷiyiṭṭa ñāṉa campantam itaḻil araciṉar kīḻtticic cuvaṭi nūlakattiṉ iraṇṭām piratiyi oṭṭip patippikkappaṭṭatu. kiḷaviyākkam toṭaṅki iṭiyiyal 10 - āvatu nūṟpā vari veḷivantatu. kallāṭar uriyit tammuṭiya tiruttaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭṭavar makāvittuvāṉ taṇṭapāṇi tēcikar. 1963 -il tolkāppiyam collatikāram urikkōvi eṉṉum peyaril āpirakām aruḷappaṉum va.ai. cuppiramaṇiyamum iṇintu veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. itu nāṉku iyalukkāṉa patippāka amintiruntatu. kaḻakap patippāka 1964 -il ku. cuntaramūrtti viḷakkavuriyuṭaṉ kallāṭam veḷivantatu.
1971 -il te.po.mī avarkaḷāl oṉpatu  piratikaḷik koṇṭu oppiṭṭu araciṉar kīḻtticic cuvaṭikaḷ nūlakap patippāka veḷivantatu. 2003 -il ti.vē. kōpāliyar, na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu tamiḻ maṇ patippakam kallāṭaṉār uriyip patippit tuḷḷatu. 2007 -il ti.vē. kōpāliyarip  patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu, caracuvati makāl nūlakam tolkāppiyam collatikāram urikkottil kallāṭar uriyi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. kallāṭar uri muḻumiyāka ituvari kiṭittilatu.
immīḷpatippil centamiḻ antaṇar avarkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa vāḻviyal viḷakkamum kā.  veḷḷivāraṇaṉāriṉ iyal viḷakkaṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi tolkāppiyattiṟkuḷ pukuvārukku eḷitāṉa maṉaniliyi uruvākkuvatuṭaṉ, āyvāḷarkaḷukku mikunta payaṉ uṭiyatāka irukkum. piṉṉiṇippāka iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭurikaḷ, tolkāppiya uriyāciriyar, urikaḷ kuṟitta nuṭpattip pulappaṭuttuvatuṭaṉ, tolkāppiyam paṟṟiya nuṇmiyi aṟiyavum tuṇi ceyyum. nūṟpā, uri vaḻik kaliccoṟkaḷ, uriyil uḷḷa mēṟkōḷ akarāti pōṉṟavi tokuttut tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyatti aṟiya virumpuvārukkum, āyvāḷarkaḷukkum  it tokuppu  payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tolkāppiyam mūlamum, uriyōṭum pala patippukaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷatu.  iruppiṉum toṭakkattil tamiḻ aṟiñar perumakkaḷāl uriyōṭu tolkāppiyattip patippitta patippukaḷ iṉṟu kiṭikkavilli. anniliyip pōkkum poruṭṭu, tamiḻariṉ paṇpāṭṭi, mēṉmiyi, uyarvip potittu vittuḷḷa nūlkaḷit tēṭit tēṭip patippikkum  tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar aiyā kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷ paḻam patippukaḷip patippikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpiyataṉ kāraṇamāka ippatippup paṇi mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. ippatippiṟku, tamiḻiṉ mūtta aṟiñar, centamiḻ antaṇar mutumuṉivar aiyā irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ patippāciriyarāka amintu eṉṉiyum ippaṇiyil iṇittukkoṇṭārkaḷ. 
 paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷip pātukāttu vittuḷḷa pulavar pallaṭam māṇikkam avarkaḷiṭam iruntu te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār tolkāppiyam collatikāram kallāṭar uri peṟappaṭṭup patippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. maṟṟa urikaḷ aṉittum  tamiḻ iṇiyap palkalikkaḻaka nūlakam, ulakat tamiḻārāycci iṇiyap pakkattil iruntu pativiṟakkam ceyyappaṭṭu im mīḷpatippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ci. kaṇēciyar patippiṉi ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻi veḷivanta ci. kaṇēciyyariṉ tolkāppiyap patippiṟku ulakat tamiḻarāycci niṟuvaṉattiṉ mēṉāḷ iyakkunar (muḻukūṭutal poṟuppu) muṉivar tiru ma. irācēntiraṉ avarkaḷ kaṇēciyar patippiṟku eḻutiyuḷḷa mukavuri avaruṭiya opputaluṭaṉ ippatippil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
muṉivar pā. matukēvaraṉ avarkaḷiṉ tolkāppiyam patippu āvaṇam 7 eṉṉum nūṟpakutiyil iruntu tol kāppiyap patippu aṭivukaḷ ippatippil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyam toṭarpāṉa palvēṟu aṟiñar  perumakkaḷiṉ āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷ tokuttu vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tolkāppiyam urikaḷ muḻutum āyvu ceytuḷḷa pērāciriyar muṉivar ca. kurucāmi avarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭurikaḷ ippatippil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  patippiṟkut tuṇi ceyta mēṟkaṇṭa aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyiṉip pulappaṭuttik koḷḷukiṟēṉ. muṉivar srī. piracāntaṉ avarkaḷ tokutta   ilakkaṇa varampu nūlilirunta kaṭṭurikaḷ kaṇēciyar patippil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. centamiḻ, tamiḻppoḻil, tamiḻiyal mutaliya āyvitaḻkaḷiliruntu tolkāppiyam toṭarpāṉa kaṭṭurikaḷ cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
patippup paṇiyil īṭupaṭuttiya aiyā tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷukkum patippil iṇintu ceyalpaṭap paṇitta aiyā mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷukkum, patippiṟkut tuṇipurinta pulavar centali kutamaṉ aiyā avarkaḷukkum eṉ neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi. taṭṭaccuc ceytu koṭutta tirumati pa. kayalviḻi  tirumati kō. cittirā. aṭṭi vaṭivamitta celvaṉ pā.  ari (hariṣ),  maṟṟum patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ tiru irā. paramēcuvaraṉ, tiru taṉacēkaraṉ, tiru ku. marutu,  tiru vi. matimāṟaṉ, ippatippiṟku utaviya eṉṉuṭiya muṉivar paṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ pā. mālati, kā. pāpu, cu. kōvintarācu, kā. kayalviḻi ākiya aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyiṉit terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ. ip  patippup paṇiyil eṉṉi muḻuvatāka īṭupaṭat tuṇiyāka niṟkum eṉ kaṇavar maruttuvar mu. cēkkiḻār avarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟiyiṉi urittākkukiṟēṉ.
kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār

nuḻivuri
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam itukāṟum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñar perumakkaḷiṉ urikaḷ yāvum paḻiya ilakkiya ilakkaṇak karuvūlattiṉ vāyilkaḷit tiṟakkiṉṟa tiṟavukōlkaḷ; tamiḻari ēṟṟiviṭum ēṇippaṭikaḷ; varalāṟṟup pātiyik kaṭakka utavum ūrtikaḷ.
tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṟivuccelvaṅkaḷi muḻu muḻu nūl tokuppukaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil perum paṅkaḷippi em patippakam ceytu varuvati ulakat tamiḻarkaḷ aṉivarum aṟivar.
tamiḻakam vēṟṟiṉattavar paṭiyeṭuppāl tākkuṇṭu atirntu nilikulintu tamiḻmakkaḷ tam marapukaḷi maṟantapōtellām paḻamperum ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkit tamiḻ marapi vāḻac ceyta perumi uriyāciriyarkaḷukku uṇṭu. 
 tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nilitta vāḻviṟkum veṟṟikkum kāraṇamāka iruppavarkaḷ uriyāciriyarkaḷē āvar. ilakkaṇak koḷkikaḷi viḷakki moḻikku varampu kaṭṭi ilakkiyak karuttukaḷi viḷakki kālantōṟum paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarttu, tamiḻ iṉattiṟkut toṇṭu ceyta perumi uriyāciriyarkaḷiyē cārum.
ovvoru uriyāciriyarum tamiḻiṉam uṟaṅkik koṇṭiruntapōtu viḻitteḻuntu, eḻuccikkural koṭutta muṟpōkkuc cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa urikaḷ yāvum kālattiṉ kuralkaḷ; camutāyattiṉ etirpārppukaḷ; tamiḻ iṉa vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷ; nerukkaṭiyiṉ veḷippāṭukaḷ ākum.
	 - uriyāciriyarkaḷ, (mu.vi.  aravintaṉ).
tolkāppiyam ilakkaṇanūl maṭṭumaṉṟu; tamiḻariṉ aṟivumarapiṉ aṭiyāḷam.  tamiḻariṉ vāḻviyali, meyyiyalip pātukātta kālappēḻi. itil potintuḷḷa tarukkaviyal kūṟukaḷ intiyat tarukkaviyal varalāṟṟiṉ mūla vaṭivaṅkaḷ
- aṟiñarkaḷiṉ pārviyil pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, (muṉivar ka. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ)
mēṟkaṇṭa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu, tolkāppiyattiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyiyum, uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttuc ceṟiviyum uḻippiyum uṇartta vallavi. ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār, va.u. citamparaṉār,  ci. kaṇēciyar, irā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi, te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār pōṉṟa peruntamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḻippāl uruvāṉa iv vāḻviyal karuvūlam tolkāppiya  urittoki eṉum peyaril  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam mīḷ patippu ceytuḷḷatu. 
2003 -ām āṇṭil em patippakam tolkāppiyatti (eḻuttu - col - poruḷ)  muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. itukāṟum veḷivantuḷḷa tolkāppiya nūl patippukaḷil  iṭam peṟāta ariya patippuc ceytikaḷ ivvurit tokiyil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivvurit toki ellā niliyilum ciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku  tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāka iruntavarkaḷip paṟṟi taṉippakkattil pativu ceytuḷḷōm.  
ivvāḻviyalnūl piḻiyaṟṟa cempatippāka veḷivaruvataṟku mutaṉmip patippāciriyar mutumuṉivar aiyā  irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār, iṇi patippāciriyar arumi makaḷ muṉivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār ākiyōriṉ uḻippum paṅkaḷippum eṉṟum maṟakka muṭiyātavi. em tamiḻ nūl patippiṟku ellā niliyilum toṭarntu ākkamum ūkkamum aḷittu varum iṉiya naṇpar pulavar centali na. kavutamaṉ avarkaḷiṉ taṉṉalam karutā tamiḻt toṇṭiṟku eṉṟum naṉṟi uṭiyēṉ.   
tamiḻarkaḷ tam illamtōṟum pātukāttu vikka vēṇṭiya ivvaruntamiḻp putiyalit tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkuvatip perumiyākak karuti makiḻkiṟōm. ippatippit tamiḻkkūṟum nallulakam ēṟṟippōṟṟum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm.
kō.  iḷavaḻakaṉ

nūlākkattiṟku utaviyōr

eḻuttatikāram 
			nacciṉārkkiṉiyam -2
ci. kaṇēciyar - patippu
					(1937, 1952)
	

mutaṟ patippu 1937lum, iraṇṭām patippu 1952lum cuṉṉākam tirumakaḷ aḻuttakattiṟ acciṭappaṭṭu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 2007il ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. ippatippukaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu mīḷ patippāka  innūl veḷivarukiṟatu.


puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal
meyyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum piṟakaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalākum. meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkumātaliṉ puḷḷiyeṉṟār. meyyīṟṟuḷ ukarampeṟuvaṉa, iṟuti keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iṟutikeṭātu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, valleḻuttum melleḻuttum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉa, iyalpāy varuvaṉa, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa, tirintu muṭivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakiyiṉuḷ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷyāvum aṭaṅku vaṉavām.
ña, na, ṇa, ma, la, ḷa eṉṉum meykaḷi iṟutiyākavuṭiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar valleḻuntu mutaṉmoḻi variṉ iru vaḻiyum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mika nilimoḻiyīṟu ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum. nakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukarampeṟātu akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, īm, kam, urum, miṉ, piṉ, kaṉ, val, tev, puḷ, vaḷ eṉa eṭuttōtiya peyarkaḷum toḻiṟpeyarpōla iruvaḻiyum ukarampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ kaṉ eṉṉuñcol vēṟṟumikkaṇ akaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikappeṟum. val eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ nāy, palaki eṉpaṉa varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avvaḻi ukaramiṉṟi akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum.
verin eṉṟa col, iṟuti nakaravoṟṟum accol peṟṟa akaramumkeṭa valleḻuttu varumvaḻi avvalleḻut tāyiṉum ataṉ kiḷiyeḻuttākiya melleḻuttāyiṉum mikku muṭiyum, makaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṟuti makaramkeṭa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉam makara vīṟukeṭṭa viṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum cila uḷa. āyiram eṉpataṉmuṉ aḷavuppeyar vantu puṇarumiṭattu vēṟṟumiyiṟpōla iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum. illam eṉṉum marappeyar iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikum. aḻaṉ eṉpataṉ iṟutikeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇvarum yaraḻa vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu avval leḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. tāy eṉṉuñcolmuṉ makaṉ viṉivariṉ valleḻuttu mikum. ār, vetir, cār, pīr, kumiḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ valleḻuttu varuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. vēṟṟumikkaṇ yakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavum cār, pāḻ eṉpaṉavum valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉavām. kīḻ eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttuppeṟṟum peṟātum uṟaḻum.
āṇ, peṇ, umaṇ, muraṇ, kuyiṉ, ekiṉ, tāṉ, pēṉ, kōṉ eṉpaṉavum neṭṭaḻuttiṉ piṉvarum lakāra, ḷakāra vīṟṟuppeyarkaḷ cilavum tiriyātu iyalpāvaṉavām. tāy eṉṉum peyarum alvaḻikkaṇvarum yakāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa. alvaḻik kaṇvarum ellārum, tām, nām, yām, tāṉ eṉpaṉa kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa. nūṟāyiram, tāmari, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉpavaṟṟōṭum ēṉi uyir mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarum, uyir mutaṉ moḻikaḷōṭum yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷum kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉavām.
āṇ, ekiṉ, pīr, pūl, vēl, āl, kumiḻ eṉpaṉa amcāriyiyum, vēṟṟumikkaṇvarum īm, kam eṉpaṉavum kōl eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum tāḻ eṉṉuñ collum, tamiḻ eṉṉum collum akkuccāriyiyum, veyil, iruḷ eṉpaṉa attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum, makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara vīṟṟu nāṭpeyarpōla āṉcāriyiyum ataṉmēl attuc cāriyiyum, ceyyuḷiṭattuvarum viṇ eṉṉuñcol viṉic col varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum, taṉittum aṭiyaṭuttum varum āyiram eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar piṟa eṇkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum peṟuvaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkippeyarum, ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilippeyarum, neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyumiyal puṭiya tām, nām, yām eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ, yāṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum cāriyipeṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum cāriyipeṟṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum, urupu puṇarccikkaṇ muṭintavāṟē vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyiṉum muṭiyumeṉa āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyāṉ vitikūṟiyuḷḷār. ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ iṭiyē vaṟṟuccāriyiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyiyum peṟum. appeyar alvaḻikkaṇ cāriyi peṟātu. uyartiṇikkaṇ iṭiyē nam cāriyiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyiyumpeṟum. vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar vaṟṟuccāriyipeṟum. ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟum.
ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl ṭakaramākavum, ṉakara lakara vīṟukaḷ ṟakaramākavum tirivaṉa. eḷḷi yuṇarttum eṇ eṉṉum peyariṉ ṇakaram alvaḻiyilum ṭakara mākattiriyum. nel, cel, kol, col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ lakaram alvaḻiyilum ṟakaramāyttiriyum. alvaḻikkaṇvarum ḷakaravīṟu tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻntu muṭiyum. vēṟṟumiyiṟpōla alvaḻiyilum ṭakaramākattiriyum ḷakara vīṟukaḷ cilavuḷa.
maṉ, ciṉ, āṉ, īṉ, piṉ, muṉ eṉpaṉavum ceyiṉ eṉṉum viṉiyeccamum avvayiṉ, ivvayiṉ, uvvayiṉ evvayiṉ eṉavarum ēḻām vēṟṟumi yiṭapporuḷuṇarttum iṭic coṟkaḷum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattiriyum. mīṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻum.
lakara ḷakara vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ takaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ āytamākattiriyum. melleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi varuṅkāl muṟiyē lakaram ṉakaramākavum ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum tirivaṉa.
makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ valivariṉ ēṟṟamelleḻuttākat tiriyum. akam eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ ki eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nilimoḻiyiṉ naṭuveḻuttākiya kakaravuyir meykeṭa melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. num eṉpataṉi alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟuṅkāl nakaravoṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaram keṭa avvoṟṟiṉmēl īkāram ūrntu nī eṉṟāki ōr ikaram iṭiyilvara iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu rakara voṟṟut tōṉṟi nīyir eṉattiriyumeṉpar tolkāppiyar. eṉavē num eṉpatē tiripil colleṉṟum nīyir eṉpatu ataṉ tiripeṉṟuṅ koḷḷutalvēṇṭum.
tēṉ eṉṉuñcol, valivariṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻtalum, iṟuti ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu valimeli mikutalum, melivariṉ iṟutikeṭṭum keṭātum uṟaḻtalum peṟum. iṟāl eṉpatu varumoḻiyākavariṉ tēṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram keṭṭut takaram iraṭṭittu muṭiyum.
cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ mutaliya iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantiyeṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ, tanti yeṉpataṉ mutaliluḷḷa takaraṅkeṭṭu, ataṉmēlēṟiya akaraṅkeṭātu niṟpa, nilimoḻiyiyaṟpeyariluḷḷa aṉkeṭṭu, aṅku niṉṟa meyyiṉmēl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa akaram ēṟimuṭiyum. muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉṉum irupeyarkaḷum varumoḻiyākiya tantiyeṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu puṇaruṅkāl ivviyaṟ peyarkaḷiṉ iṟutiniṉṟa aṉkeṭa eñciya takaravoṟṟum, tanti eṉpatil muṉkūṟiyapaṭi takaraṅkeṭa eñciya akaravuyirum cērakkeṭṭu, ānti, pūnti eṉa muṭivaṉavām. ippeyarkaḷ ciṟappup paṇpaṭuttuvaruṅkāl aṅṅaṉantiritaliṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa eṉpar. mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ iṉṉāṟku makaṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉṉum muṟiyil oṭṭi niṟkuṅkāl nilimoḻip peyarīṟṟiṉ aṉkeṭṭu amcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
muṉ eṉṉum colmuṉ il eṉṉuñcol vantu puṇariṉ iṭiyē ṟakaravoṟṟut tōṉṟi muṉṟil eṉamuṭital toṉṟu toṭṭu maruvivaḻaṅkum ilakkaṇa muṭipām. poṉ eṉṉuñcol, īṟṟil ṉakaramkeṭa lakaravuyirmeyyum makara meyyumpeṟṟup polam eṉat tirintuvaḻaṅkum. il eṉṉum col, vīṭṭi yuṇarttātu illāmiyeṉṉum poruḷi yuṇarttuṅkāl, aikāram peṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum, mikāmiyum, iyalpātalum, ākārampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikutalum ākiya nāṉkumuṭipiṉi yuṭiyatākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 181-185

eṭṭāvatu
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal
296.		ñakāri yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉa
					rallatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
					valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē
					yukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram.
niṟutta muṟiyāṉē uyirīṟu puṇarttup puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉ kaṇattōṭuñ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ ivvōttup puḷḷimayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram - ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻiyum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar - ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṉṟāka niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumiyic collumiṭattum, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻuttumikum-valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varu moḻiyāy variṉ avvalleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉ āṉa ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram varuka eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: uriñukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, uriñuk kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.	(1)
297.	ñanamava viyiyiṉu mukara niliyum.
iḵtu avvīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattōṭum muṭiyumeṉa eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:ñanamava iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum - aññakara īṟu vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ña na ma va mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum. eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	uriñuñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.
yakarattōṭum uyirōṭum puṇarumāṟu tokimarapiṉuḷ 1ukara moṭu puṇarum (eḻu. 163) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.	(2)
298.	nakara viṟutiyu mataṉō raṟṟē.
itu, nakara īṟu muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟi eytāta teytuvikkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nakara iṟutiyum - nakara īṟṟuppeyarum muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, ataṉōraṟṟu - aññakara īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avve ḻuttu mikku ukaram peṟṟum ña na ma va vantuḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	porunukkaṭitu verinukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum. muṭipu oppumi nōkki nakarīṟu īṇṭup puṇarttār. 2īṇṭu vēṟṟumi yoḻittu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka.		(3)
299.	vēṟṟumik kukkeṭa vakara niliyum.
itu, nilimoḻi ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ ey tiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikku - annakara īṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikku, ukkeṭa akaram niliyum - mēleytiya ukaraṅ keṭa akarattōṭu nilipeṟṟup puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	porunakkaṭumi verinakkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉa varum. akara niliyum eṉṉātu ukaraṅkeṭa eṉṟataṉāṉ uru piṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvuñ ciṟupāṉmi ukarappēṟuṅ koḷka. veriniṉkuṟi poruniṉkuṟi uriñiṉkuṟi eṉavum, uyaval yāṉi verinuc ceṉṟaṉṉa (akam. 658) eṉavum varum. yāṉiyiṉatu mutukiṉmēṟ ceṉṟaṉṉa eṉa virikka.
3porun eṉpatu oru cātippeyarum, poruntutal leṉṉum viṉippeyarumām.	(4)
300.		verine ṉiṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
					varumiṭa ṉuṭittē melleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu annakara īṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: verin eṉ iṟuti - verin eṉṟu collappaṭum nakara īṟṟumoḻi, muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi - taṉīṟṟu nakaram muṉpeṟṟa ukarattōṭu eñcāmik keṭṭa iṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟki varum iṭaṉ uṭittu - melleḻuttup peṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: veriṅkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum.
melleḻuttu varumoḻi nōkki vantatu. veyilveri niṟutta payilitaḻp pacuṅkuṭi eṉpatil 4nakara ikaramē iṭṭeḻutupa.	(5)
301.		āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.
iḵtu - ataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āvayiṉ - avverin eṉṉuñ col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟa iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu – melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: verikkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum.		(6)
302.		ṇakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyipiṉ
					ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ṇakāra īṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭkaṭ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṇakāra iṟuti - ṇakāra īṟṟuppeyar, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ, ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭku - ṭakāramākat tirintu muṭiyum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: maṭkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum. maṇki puṇki eṉpaṉa 5iraṇṭāvataṉ tiripiṉ muṭintaṉa.
kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉpaṉa mēl muṭittum.	(7)
303.	āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟu viravuppeyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āṇum peṇṇum - āṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum peṇṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum, aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki - toki marapiṉuḷ moḻi mutalākum (eḻut. 147) eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇip peyar muṭinta iyalpupōlat tāmum vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: āṇki  peṇki  cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
itu tokimarapiṉuḷ aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar (eḻu. 155) eṉpataṉuḷ muṭinta iyalpaṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivi āṇṭu muṭintaṉa pōlat tattam marapiṉ viṉiyāṟ pālaṟiyap paṭuvaṉa aṉṟi 6iru tiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ, aḵṟiṇip peyaratu iyalpōṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittā reṉka. āṇkaṭitu peṇkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipu moḻi mutalākum (eḻu. 147) eṉpataṉkaṇ varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavata ṉāṉ muṭikka.	(8)
304.	āṇmarak kiḷavi yarimara viyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āṇmarak kiḷavi - āṇpāli uṇarttātu āṇeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col, ari mara iyaṟṟu - ariyeṉṉum maram ammuppeṟṟa iyalpiṟṟāyt tāṉum ammup peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	āṇaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka. āṇa nār ili eṉa varum.
  7viravuppeyaraṉṟeṉṟaṟku marameṉṟār.	(9)
305.		viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayi
					ṉuṇmiyu murittē yatteṉ cāriyi
					ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṟ ṟoḻilvaru kāli.
itu, ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: viṇ eṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ - viṇṇeṉṟu collavarukiṟa ākāyatti uṇara niṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyi mikutalum urittu - atteṉṉuñ cāriyi uṇṭātalum urittu illiyātalum urittu; ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻil varukāli - ceyyuḷiṭattut toḻiṟcol varuṅ kālattu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	viṇṇattuk koṭkum vaṇṇat tamarar viṇṇattuk koṭkum viricel lūrtiyōy eṉavum, viṇkuttu nīḷ vari veṟpa kaḷipavō (nālaṭi. 226) eṉavum varum. viṇṇeṉṉum kuṟippiṉi nīkkutaṟkuk kāyam eṉṟār.
viṇvattuk koṭkum eṉa uṭampaṭumey puṇarntu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. 8atikāra valleḻuttiṉmiyiṟ cāriyi valleḻuttuk koṭukka.	(10)
306.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vitikkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟ peyar iyala -  alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyaratu iyalpiṉavāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	9maṇṇukkaṭitu paṇṇukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
ellā meṉṟataṉāl toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum iṉcāriyi peṟṟum puṇarvaṉa koḷka.
utāraṇam: veṇṇukkari, tāḻpeyal kaṉikural kaṭuppap paṇṇup peyarttu eṇṇuppāṟu, veṇṇiṉkari eṉa varum.	(11)
307.	kiḷippeya rellāṅ koḷattiri pilavē.
iḵtu ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷip peyarellām - ṇakāra īṟṟuḷ ōriṉatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām, koḷat tiripu ila - tiripuṭiya veṉṟu karutumpaṭiyākat tiritalilavāy iyalpāy  muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	umaṇ eṉa niṟuttik kuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉat tantu muṭikka.
iṉi, ellā meṉṟataṉāṟ, piṟa cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉa vum, iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. 10maṇṇappattam eṇṇa noli eṉavum, kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉavuṅ koḷka.
koḷa eṉṟataṉāl, ēḻām vēṟṟumi poruṇmi uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ tirintu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. aṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ uṅkaṭ koṇ ṭāṉ eṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ īṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkaṭ koṇ ṭāṉ eṉavum, avaṭkoṇṭāṉ ivaṭkoṇṭāṉ uvaṭkoṇ ṭāṉ evaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(12)
308.		vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yeṇṇe ṉuṇavuppeyar
					vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumi muṭipu pōlat tirintu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - vēṟṟumiyallāta iṭattu, eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar - variyaṟip poruṇmi uṇarttātu eṇṇeṉṟu collappaṭum uṇaviṉi uṇarttum peyar, vēṟṟumi iyaṟki niliyalum urittu - vēṟṟumiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	 eṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. ummi yāl, tokimarapiṉuḷ 11moḻimutalākum (eḻu. 147) eṉṟataṉāṟ kūṟiya iyalpu perumpāṉmiyāyiṟṟu. aḵtu eṇkaṭitu eṉa varum.	(13)
309.	muraṇeṉ ṟoḻiṟpeyar mutaliya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟuṭ toḻiṟpeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut toḻiṟ peyarkku eytiya ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumimuṭipum eytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar - māṟupāṭu uṇarttum muraṇeṉṉun toḻiṟpeyar, mutaliyal niliyum - 12toki marapiṟ kūṟiya alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  muraṇkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, muraṭkaṭumi cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, muraṇñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum. itaṉit toḻiṟpeyarellām (eḻut. 306) eṉṟataṉ piṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, muraṇkaṭumi muraṭkaṭumi, araṇkaṭumi araṭkaṭumi eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuṅ koḷka.		(14)
310.		makara viṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṟ
					ṟuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṭ puṇaru māṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: makara iṟuti vēṟṟumi āyiṉ - makara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum - annilimoḻi makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  marakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. muṇṭakak kōti eṉavum varum. tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyalpu kaṇattuk kaṇṇum uyar tiṇip peyarkkaṇṇum 13viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka. marañāṇ maranūl ivaṟṟiṟku nāṉkaṉurupu virikka. mara maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka. naṅki eṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, taṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum varum. īṇṭu makarakkēṭē koḷka; muṭipu mēṟkūṟupa (eḻu. 320).	(15)
311.		akara ākāram varūuṅ kāli
					yīṟṟumici yakara nīṭalu murittē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: akara ākāram varūum kāli - akara mutaṉmoḻiyum ākāra mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, īṟṟu mici akaram nīṭalum urittu - makara oṟṟiṉ mēl niṉṟa akaram nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu nīṭāmiyum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	 maram kuḷam eṉa niṟutti, makaraṅkeṭuttu, aṭi āmpal eṉat tantu, rakara ḷakaraṅkaḷil niṉṟa akaram ākāra mākki, marāaṭi kuḷāampal eṉa muṭikka.
mēṟ celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa (eḻu. 312) eṉpata ṉāṟ, kuḷāampal eṉpuḻi āmpal eṉpataṉ ākāratti akara mākkuka. 
ummiyāṉ, maravaṭi kuḷavāmpal eṉa nīṭāmiyuṅ koḷka. 
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟup piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka. 	kōṇākōṇam kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉa varum. ivaṟṟiṟku uḷḷeṉṉum urupu virikka.
kōṇākōṇattiṟku valleḻuttukkēṭu 14mēlic cūttirattu ilēcāṟ koḷka.	(16)
312.		melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
					celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
itu, makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikutalōṭu melle ḻuttum uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa - makara īṟṟuḷ valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttup peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, vaḻakkattu āṉa celvaḻi aṟital  -  vaḻakkattiṉ kaṇ vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	kuḷaṅkari kuḷakkari cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ kuḷaṅkari kuḷakkaripōla allaṉa otta uṟaḻc ciyāy vaḻaṅkā veṉṟaṟkuc celvaḻiyaṟital eṉṟār. vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷattuk koṇ ṭāṉ īḻattuc ceṉṟāṉ kuṭattuvāy pilattuvāy eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa, makaraṅ keṭṭu attuppeṟṟaṉa. ivi attē vaṟṟē (eḻu. 133) eṉpataṉāṉ 15oṟṟuk keṭāvāyiṉa, aḵtu alvaḻikkuk kūṟutaliṉ. maḻakaḷiṟu eṉpatu maḻavuṅ kuḻavu miḷamip poruḷa eṉṟa uriccol. atu makara īṟaṉṟu. caṇpakaṅkōṭu eṉpatu vaḻakkiṭattuc cellātu. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, makaraṅkeṭātu niṟpaṉavuṅ koḷka. pulampuk kaṉaṉē (puṟam. 258) kalampeṟu kaṇṇuḷa rokkaṟ ṟaliva (malipaṭu. 50) eṉa varum.	(17)
313.	illa marappeyar vicimara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melle ḻuttu vitittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: illa marappeyar - pukku uṟiyum illaṉṟi illa meṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col, vici mara iyaṟṟu - vici yeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	illaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattu vaḻakkattāṉa (eḻu. 312) eṉṟataṉāṉ, makarakkēṭu koḷka.	(18)
314.	alvaḻi yellā melleḻut tākum.
itu, makaram alvaḻikkaṇ tirika eṉa muṟkūṟāmiyiṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:alvaḻi yellām melleḻut 
tākum - makara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	maraṅkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. maram peritu eṉpuḻit tiripiṉṟeṉpatu 16āṇi kūṟaleṉṉum utti.
iṉi ellā meṉṟataṉāl, alvaḻikkaṇ makara īṟu piṟa vāṟṟāṉ muṭivaṉavellām muṭikka. vaṭṭattaṭukku, caturap palaki, āytap puḷḷi, vēḻakkarumpu, kalakkoḷ, cukku, tōri, payaṟu; nīlakkaṇ eṉṉum paṇput tokikkaṇ makaraṅ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭintaṉa. āyuta vulakki, akara mutala  17ivi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅ keṭṭu muṭintaṉa. ellāruṅ kuṟiyar nāṅkuṟiyēm: ivi uyartiṇip peyar makaran tirintu melleḻuttāy muṭintaṉa. kolluṅ koṟṟaṉ, uṇṇuñ cāttaṉ, kavaḷa māntu malikeḻu nāṭaṉ, poru māraṉ, tāvupari, paṟakkunāri, ōṭunākam, āṭupōr, varukālam, kollum yāṉi, pāṭumpāṇaṉ eṉa ivi makaran tirintum keṭṭum nili peṟṟum vanta peyareccam.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum makaraṅ keṭutaluṅ keṭāmiyuṅ koḷka. marañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, maramyātu valitu aṭintatu eṉavum varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pavaḷavāyeṉa uvamattum, nilanīreṉa eṇṇiṭattum kēṭu koḷka.	(19)
315.		akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kimuṉ variṉē
					mutaṉili yoḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum
					varinili yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka
					melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, makara īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ immoḻi immuṭipu eytuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytātateytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:akam eṉ kiḷavikkuk ki muṉvariṉ - akameṉṉuñ colliṟkuk kiyeṉṉuñ colmuṉṉē varumāyiṉ, mutaṉili oḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum - muṉṉiṉṟa akaraṅ keṭātu niṟpa ataṉ muṉ niṉṟa kakaramum makaravoṟṟuṅ keṭṭu muṭitalum keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum, vari nili iṉṟē yāciriyarkka - nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku, āvayiṉāṉ melleḻuttu mikutal - avi keṭṭavaḻi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	aṅki eṉa varum. akaṅki eṉak keṭātu muṭintavaḻi 18alvaḻi ellām (eḻu. 314) eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaran tirintu muṭital koḷka. itu paṇputtoki. 19atikārattāṉum poruṇōkkāṉum vēṟṟumit tokiyaṉmi uṇarka.(20)
316.		ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kāli
					niliyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.
iḵtu ilameṉpatu muṟṟuviṉic collākātu kuṟippākiya uriccollāy niṟkuṅkāl alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyu māṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ilam eṉ kiḷavikku - illāmi yeṉṉum kuṟippākiya uriccoṟku, paṭu varu kāli - uṇṭātaleṉṉum poruḷpaṭum 20viṉik kuṟippuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, ceyyuḷāṉa niliyalum urittu -  ceyyuḷiṭattu makarak, kēṭun tiripumiṉṟi niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ummiyāṟ piṟacol varuṅkālattuk kēṭun tiripum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uritteṉak koḷka.
utāraṇam: 	ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟaki niṟiya eṉa varum. itaṟku illāmi uṇṭākiṉṟa pulavareṉap poruḷ kūṟuka. ilampāṭu nāṇut tarum  eṉkiṉṟatō veṉiṉ, illāmi uṇṭātal nāṇuttaru meṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuka. itaṉi neṟpāṭu periteṉṟāṟ pōlak koḷka. itu poruḷilameṉa muṟṟuviṉic collāmāṟum uṇarka. ila niṉṟateṉak keṭṭavāṟum, ilaṅkeṭa viyantāṉ ilañciṟitāka ilantīteṉṟu eṉak kacatakkaḷ varumvaḻit tirintavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
ellā (eḻu. 314) meṉṟataṉāṉ ilam varuvatu pōlum, ilam yāriṭattu eṉa vakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉark keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka.
itaṉi ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉa vēṟṟumi yeṉṟārāl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ, paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉpatu peyareccamāta liṟ paṟṟaveṉṉun toḻil tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa mutaṉilic collic cūttirattu āciriyar eṭuttōtiṟṟi larātalāṉum, paṭuveṉpatu tāṉum pulavareṉṉum peyarōṭu muṭiyuṅkāl kālam kāṭṭum īṟukaḷ tokka mutaṉilic collāy niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi eṭuttōti ṉārātalāṉum, āciriyarkku aṅṅaṉam kūṟutal karuttaṉmi uṇarka. 
aṉṟiyum paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉpuḻi iraṇṭu mutaṉili kūṭi oṉṟāy niṉṟu paṟṟutalic ceyyappaṭṭa eṉap poruḷ tārāmiyāṉum, alvaḻi yatikāramātalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmi uṇarka.	(21)
317.		attoṭu civaṇum māyirat tiṟuti
					yotta veṇṇu muṉvaru kāli.
iḵtu  ivvīṟṟu eṇṇup peyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut toki marapiṉuḷ uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāki (eḻut. 164) eṉpataṉāṉ eytiya ēyeṉ cāriyi vilakki attu vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āyirattu iṟuti - āyirameṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ makaram, otta eṇṇu muṉvarukāli - taṉakku akappaṭum moḻiyāyp poruntiṉa eṇṇuppeyar taṉ muṉ varum kālattu, attoṭu civaṇum - tokimarapiṟ kūṟiya ēyeṉ cāriyi oḻittu attuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu..
utāraṇam:  āyirattoṉṟu, āyirattoṉpatu eṉa oṉṟu mutal oṉpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. makaratti attiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli (eḻut. 125) eṉṟu muṭikka. āyirat torupatu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oṭṭuka.
nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, āyirattukkuṟi kūṟu mutal eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, āyirappatteṉpuḻima karaṅkeṭuttu valloṟṟu mikuttu muṭikka.(22)
318.	aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilik kuriya 110) eṉṟamiyiṉ, avvaṇṇup peyari aṭikiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum - avvāyira meṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṭiyaṭutta moḻiyōṭu variṉum, ataṉōraṟṟu - muṟkūṟiyataṉōṭu orutaṉmittāy attup peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu iraṇṭu, irupatiṉā yirattoṉṟu, āṟāyirattoṉṟu, nūṟāyirattoṉṟu, munnūṟāyirat toṉṟu, ainnūṟāyirattoṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. muṉṉar ilēciṉāṉ muṭintavaṟṟiyum aṭiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka. patiṉāyirattukkuṟi kūṟu mutal eṉavum, nūṟāyirappattu eṉavum varum.	(23)
319.		aḷavu niṟiyum vēṟṟumi yiyala.
iḵtu - avveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum vantāl muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyum - atikārattāl MyirantāṉēÃṉṟuḻiyummṭiyaṭuttuÃṉṟuḻiyummḷavupbpayarumÃṟipbpayarumtntāl,nvaṟṟumiïyala- kkara<ṟṟunvaṟṟumiyōṭuxttutlleḻuttutntuḻikkaraṅkeṭṭutlleḻuttuÄkkum,ïyalpukaṇamtntuḻi‘tuvara(eḻu.310) eṉṉum ilēcāṉ eytiya makaraṅkeṭṭum puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: āyiram patiṉāyiram üṟāyiram eṉa niṟuttikkalam caṭitūtipaṉinaḻimaṇṭivaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum ,kaḵcukaḻañcu toṭitulāmpalam eṉavun tantu oṭṭuka .vēṟṟumi yiyala’ eṉavē, tām vēṟṟumiyallavāyiṉa.(24)

320.		paṭarkkip peyaru muṉṉilip peyarun
					toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilik kiḷaviyum
					vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉurupiya ṉiliyu
					melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu uyartiṇippeyarum viravuppeyarum urupiyaluḷ muṭintavāṟē īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭikaveṉa eytātatu eytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum - ellārumeṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarum ellīru meṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarum, toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilik kiḷaviyum - kiḷit toṭarccip poruḷavāy neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyuntām nām yāmeṉṉum peyarākiya nilimiyuṭiya collum, vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik mēkaṇṇāyiṉ urupu puṇarccikkuk kūṟiya iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, MvayiṉāṉbmalleḻuttuÄkutal- mēl bnaṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu utāraṇam:  ellāru meṉpataṉi makara oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭuttu, rakarap puḷḷiyi niṟuttik, ki cevi tali puṟam eṉattantu, iṭiyilē tammuccāriyiyum iṟutiyilē ummuc cāriyiyum koṭuttu, ellār taṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka. itaṟku, ammiṉiṟuti (eḻu. 129) eṉṉum cūttirattuḷ taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa cāriyikkaṇ makara oṟṟut tirintu ṅañanavākum eṉac ceyki ceytu muṭikka. ellīru meṉpataṟku, iṭiyilē nummuc cāriyiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāriyiyum koṭuttu, muṟkūṟiya ceyki kaḷellāñ ceytu, ellīr nuṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka. tām, nām eṉpaṉavaṟṟi ēṉi yiraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum (eḻu. 188) eṉak kuṟukki, makaraviṟuti (eḻu. 310) eṉpataṉkaṇ tuvara eṉpataṉāṉ makaraṅkeṭuttut, taṅki naṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka. yām eṉpataṉi ākāratti ekaramākki yakara oṟṟik keṭuttu makaraviṟuti (eḻu. 310) eṉpataṉkaṇ tuvara eṉṟata ṉāṉ makaraṅ keṭuttu, eṅki cevi tali puṟamvṉamuṭikf. toṭakkaṅ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku iccūttirattāṉ melleḻuttu mikukka.
urupiyal niliyum eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumiyātal peṟā niṟkavum, piṉṉum vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum iyalpu kaṇattu ñakāramum nakāramum vantuḻit tammuc cāriyiyum, nummuc cāriyiyum peṟutalum, āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaraṅkeṭṭu ummup peṟutalum oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum koḷka. 	ellār taññāṇum, ellīr nuññāṇum nūlum eṉa varum.
iṉit toṭakkaṅ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum 21avviraṇṭu ilēcāṉum makaraṅkeṭutalum oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum koḷka. taññāṇ, naññāṇ, eññāṇ nūl eṉa varum.
iṉṉum āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉē, ellārtam  ellīr num eṉa niṉṟavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar ,ēṉi maṇiyaḻvaṭṭu aṭi eṉpaṉa vantuḻi makaraṅ bkaṭāmiyum ummuppeṟutaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, toṭakkaṅ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkun tammaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa makaraṅ keṭāmiyuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, 22tamakāṇam namakāṇam emakāṇam numakāṇam eṉa urupīṟṟuc ceykikaḷuṅ koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, 23num eṉpataṟku makaratti melloṟṟākki nuṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varutalum, nuññāṇ eṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, numvali eṉa makaram keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ellārkiyum ellīrkiyum eṉat tammum nummum peṟātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.	(25)
321.	allatu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
itu, muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkiyākum - ammūṉṟu pe yarum alvaḻiyic collumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
īṇṭu iyaṟki yeṉṟatu cāriyi peṟāmi nōkki. ivaṟṟiṉ īṟutirital alvaḻi yellām (eḻu. 314) eṉpataṉuḷ ellā meṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
utāraṇam:  ellāruṅkuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, ellīruṅ kuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉavum, tāṅkuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, tāṅkuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉavum, nāṅkuṟiyam ciṟiyam tīyam periyam eṉavum, yāṅkuṟiyēm ciṟiyēm tīyēm periyēm eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ellā meṉṟataṉāṉē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar ñakāra nakāram vantāl avi avvoṟṟāyt tirital koḷka. ellāruñ ñāṉṟār nīṇṭār, ellīruñ ñāṉṟīr nīṇṭīr eṉavum, tāññāṉṟār nīṇṭār eṉavum, nāññāṉṟām nīṇṭām eṉavum, yāñ ñāṉṟēm nīṇṭēm eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ellārum vantār yāttār aṭintār, ellīrum vantīr yāttīr aṭintīr eṉavum, tām vantār yāttār aṭintār eṉavum, nām varutum yāttum aṭitum eṉavum, yām varuvēm yāppēm aṭivēm eṉavum ēṉikkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar makarantiriyātu niṟṟal, uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum (eḻu. 153) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum	.(26)
322.		allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu
					mellā meṉumpeya rurupiya ṉiliyum
					vēṟṟumi yalvaḻic cāriyi niliyātu.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum urupiyalōṭu māṭṭeṟintu eytātateytu vittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum - alvaḻikkaṭ colliṉum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṭ colliṉum, ellāmeṉum peyar urupiyal niliyum - ellā meṉṉum viravup peyar urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumi yalvaḻic cāriyi niliyātu - appeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyallāta iṭattu vaṟṟuc cāriyi nillātāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
urupiya ṉiliyum eṉṟa māṭṭēṟu, alvaḻikkaṇ ummup peṟṟu niṟṟalum, poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uṇarttiṟṟu.
ummuccāriyi oṉṟumē peṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa alvaḻiyiṉi yum, vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa vēṟṟumiyōṭu uṭaṉōti, atuvum vaṟṟup peṟumāṟu pōla māṭṭeṟinta mikiyāṉē, vaṉkaṇattu alvaḻikkaṇ nilimoḻi makarak kēṭum, varumoḻi valleḻuttup pēṟum, īṟṟummip pēṟum, meṉkaṇattu makaraṅ keṭṭu ummup peṟṟum peṟātum varutalum, ēṉikkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu ummup peṟṟum, makaraṅ keṭātu ummup peṟātum varutaluṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam:  ellākkuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉavum, ellā ñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ellā ñāṉṟaṉa nīṇṭaṉa māṇṭaṉa eṉavum, ellā yāḻum vaṭṭum aṭiyum eṉavum, ellām vāṭiṉa āṭiṉa eṉavum varum. iṉi, vēṟṟumikkaṇ ellāvaṟṟuk kōṭum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa ivi vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku makaram vaṟṟiṉ mici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukka. iṉi, meṉkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟu ñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ēṉik kaṇattukkaṇellāvaṟ¿yāppu«vaḻiyu«aṭiyu«eṉavu«varum. ēṉik kaṇamum vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa.	(27)
323.	melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.
iḵtu orucār valleḻutti vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli - avvellā meṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ mēl ilēciṉāṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟa milli eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, valleḻuttu mikutalē perumpāṉmiyāyiṟṟu. muṟkūṟiya ceykimēlē itu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, makarak kēṭum ummuppēṟuṅ koḷka.
	ellāṅ kuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉa varum.
māṉamilli eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku iṟuti ummup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu ummup peṟṟu muṭitaluṅ koḷka. ellāk kollarum cāṉṟārum taccarum pārppārum kuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉavum, ellā ñāṉṟārum nāykarum maṇiyakārarum vaṇikarum aracarum eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, uyartiṇikkaṇ ellāṅkuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikku ummup peṟutalum, ellāṅ kuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, 24kuṟiyīr kuṟiyam eṉavum ummup peṟātu varutaluṅ koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iṭikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum makaraṅ keṭātu ummiṉṟi varutaluṅ koḷka. ellām vantēm aṭintēm eṉa varum.	(28)
324.	uyartiṇi yāyi ṉurupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu - ellā meṉpataṟku uyartiṇi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:uyartiṇiyāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum - ellā meṉpatu uyartiṇiyāy niṟkumāyiṉ urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iṭikkaṇ nammum iṟutikkaṇ ummum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu..
urupiyaluḷ ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar - vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi (eḻu. 189) vakuttataṉāṉ, vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟ ṟeṉṟu makaraṅ keṭutta atikārattāṉ uyartiṇi yāyi ṉammiṭi varum (eḻu. 190) eṉa nammiṉ muṉṉum makaraṅ keṭuttār; ata ṉōṭu īṇṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ. atu koṇṭu īṇṭum makaraṅ keṭukka. ammiṉiṟuti (eḻu. 129) eṉpuḻit taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṉ nammuccāriyiyiṉatu makaran tirital koḷka.
utāraṇam: 	ellā naṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟaliṉ, ellā naññāṟciyum nīṭci yum eṉa ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu muṭipu aṟintu oṭṭuka.	(29)
325.	numme ṉorupeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu, makara īṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ oru peyar melleḻuttu mikum - nummeṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa viravuppeyar poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nuṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum. makaraviṟuti (eḻu. 310) eṉpatāṉ makaraṅkeṭukka. 
oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ, uṅki eṉa varuvatūuṅ koḷka. tuvara (eḻu. 310) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ñakara nakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻi makaraṅ keṭutalum, oru peyar eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟu mikutaluṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam:	nuññāṇ nūl eṉa varum. iṉṉum oru peyar eṉṟataṉāṉ, nummaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa makaraṅ keṭāmiyum koḷka.	(30)
326.		allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅ kāli
					yukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayi ṉīvara
					iṭi nilii yīṟukeṭa rakara
					niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
					yappāṉ moḻivayi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
itu, nummeṉpataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅkāli - nummeṉpa taṉi alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟumiṭattu, u keṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ ī vara - nakara vukarattuḷ ukaraṅkeṭṭup pōka oḻintu niṉṟa nakara voṟṟiṭattē īkāram vantu niṟpa, i iṭinilii īṟukeṭa  -  ōr ikaram iṭiyilē vantu nilipeṟṟu makaramākiya īṟu keṭṭuppōka, puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntu rakaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum - āṇṭup puḷḷi peṟṟu oru rakaram vantu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ, appāl moḻivayiṉ - ak kūṟṟiṉi yuṭiya nili moḻiyiṭattu, iyaṟkiyākum - varuñcol iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  	 nīyir kuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉa varum.
colluṅkāli yeṉṟataṉāṉē, nīyir ñāṉṟīr; nīṇṭīr, māṇṭīr, yāttīr, vāṭiṉīr, aṭintīr eṉa ēṉikkaṇattilum oṭṭuka.			(31)
327.		toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
itu, vēṟṟumikkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkum alvaḻikkaṇ melleḻuttāyt tirintum varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki ñakara īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla niṟkumeṉap piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām - makara īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar pōla alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:    	 25cemmuk kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, cemmukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum. tummuc ceṟuppa  eṉpatum atu. ivi kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru viraṭṭal (eḻu. 160) 
ellā meṉṟataṉāṉ, ukaram peṟātu 26nāṭṭaṅkaṭitu āṭṭaṅ kaṭitu eṉa melleḻuttāy alvaḻikkaṇ tiritalum, nāṭṭakkaṭumi āṭṭakkaṭumi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ valle ḻuttu mikutaluṅ koḷka.	(32)
328.		īmuṅ kammu murumeṉ kiḷaviyu
					māmup peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa.
itu, poruṭpeyaruṭ cila, toḻiṟ peyarōṭu ottu muṭika eṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:īmuṅ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum ā muppeyarum - īmeṉṉuñ colluṅ kammeṉṉuñ collum urumeṉṉuñ collumākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, avaṟṟō raṉṉa - muṟkūṟiya toḻiṟ peyarōṭu oru taṉmiyavāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
īm eṉpatu cuṭukāṭu; kam eṉpatu toḻil.
utāraṇam: 	īmukkaṭitu, kammuk kaṭitu, urumukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, īmukkaṭumi, kammukkaṭumi, urumukkaṭumi, ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum oṭṭuka.
kiḷavi yeṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum alvaḻik kaṇṇum uyir varuvaḻi ukaram peṟātu, īmaṭivu, īmaṭintatu eṉa niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. 
taṉṉiṉamuṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ, ammu tammu nammu eṉac cāriyik kaṇṇum ukaram varutal koḷka.	(33)
329.		vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yiraṇṭun
					tōṟṟam vēṇṭu makkeṉ cāriyi.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṟku vēṟṟu mikkaṇ vēṟōr muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, ēṉi iraṇṭum tōṟṟam vēṇṭum akkeṉ cāriyi - iṟutiyil urumoḻinta iraṇṭum akkeṉṉuñ cāriyi tōṉṟi muṭitali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum, ñāṟci neruppu māṭci viṟaku eṉavum oṭṭuka.
akku vakuppavē, nilimoḻit toḻilākiya ukaraṅ keṭṭu, 27muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu vilakkappaṭāmiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu.
vaṉkaṇattiṟku muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattiṟ kūṟiyatu 28kuṇa vēṟṟumik keṉṟum, īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu poruṭpuṇarccik keṉṟuṅ koḷka.	(34)


330.		vakāra miciyu makāraṅ kuṟukum.
itu, muṉṉar ariyaḷavu kuṟukal (eḻu. 13) eṉavum ṉakāri muṉṉar (eḻu. 52) eṉavuṅ kūṟiya makaram irumoḻik kaṇṇuṅ kuṟukumeṉa, ataṉ īṟṟakattu eytāta teytuvik kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vakāramiciyum makāraṅ kuṟukum - makāram oru moḻik kaṇṇēyaṉṟi vakārattiṉ mēluṅ kuṟukum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	nilam valitu, varum vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa varum.		(35)
331.		nāṭpeyark kiḷavi mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa
					vattu māṉmici varinili yiṉṟē
					yoṟṟumey keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi mēṟ kiḷantaṉṉa - makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarpōla āṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum, attum āṉmici varinili iṉṟu - attuc cāriyi āṉcāriyi mēlum piṟa cāriyi mēlum varutal nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu, oṟṟu meykeṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - āṇṭu nilimoḻi makara oṟṟuk keṭuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
ummiyi āṉmici meṉa māṟuka.
utāraṇam: makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ōṇattāṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉka. ēṉi nāḷkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. makara oṟṟukkeṭuttu atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli (eḻu. 125) eṉa akaraṅkeṭuttuk, kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa  (eḻu. 105) eṉa āṉēṟṟi, āṉi ṉakaramum, (eḻu. 124) eṉṟataṉāṉ ṟakaramākki muṭikka. makattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyi ṉāṉ eṉa ñāṉṟeṉṉuñ cāriyimēl attu vantatu.
variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, immuṭipu nāṉku kaṇattuṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam: 	makattāṉ ñāṟṟiṉāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māyntāṉ, vantāṉ, aṭintāṉ eṉa varum.	(36)
332.		ṉakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
					ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ṉakara iṟuti vēṟṟumikkaṭ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṟakāra mākum - ṉakāra īṟṟup peyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyiyiṉ ṟakāramākum, vēṟṟumip poruṭku - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: poṟkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum 					(37)
333.		maṉṉuñ ciṉṉu māṉu mīṉum
					piṉṉu muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
					maṉṉa viyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu  avvīṟṟu acinili . ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷuṇara niṉṟa iṭic caṟkaḷum, viṉiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉporuḷ: maṉṉuñ ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - maṉṉeṉṉuñ collum ciṉṉeṉṉuñ collum āṉeṉṉuñ collum īṉeṉṉuñ collum piṉṉeṉṉuñ collum muṉeṉṉuñ collum viṉiyeccamākiya collum, aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar - muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉi yuṭiyavāy ṉakaram ṟakaramāy muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	atumaṟ koṇkaṉ ṟērē kāppum pūṇṭi ciṟ kaṭiyum pōkal (akam. 7) eṉavum, āṟkoṇṭāṉ īṟkoṇṭāṉ piṟkoṇṭāṉ muṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, variṟ koḷḷum cellum tarum pōm eṉavum varum.
peyarāntaṉmiyavākiya 29āṉ īṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi muṟ kūṟātataṉāṉ, āṉ koṇṭāṉ īṉ koṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka. 
piṉ muṉ eṉpaṉa, peyarum urupum viṉiyeccamumāy niṟṟaliṉ peyar īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ēṉiya, urupiyaluḷḷum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi yeṉpataṉ kaṇṇum muṭiyum. 30appeyari muṟkūṟātataṉāṟ, piṉkoṇṭāṉ muṉ koṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka.
iyalaveṉṟataṉāṉ ūṉeṉṉuñ cuṭṭu ūṉ koṇṭā ṉeṉa iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.	(38)
334.		cuṭṭumutal vayiṉu mekaramutal vayiṉu
					mappaṇpu niliyu miyaṟkiya veṉpa.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporu ḷuṇarttum iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutal vayiṉum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya vayiṉeṉṉuñ collum, ekaramutal vayiṉum - ekara mākiya mutal viṉāviṉiyuṭiya vayiṉeṉṉuñ collum, ap paṇpu niliyum iyaṟki eṉpa - mēl ṉakaram ṟakaramā meṉṟa taṉmi nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: avvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, ivvayiṟ koṇṭāṉ, uvvayiṟ koṇṭāṉ, evvayiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iyaṟkiya eṉṟataṉāl, tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(39)
335.	kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.
itu,  ṉakāran tiriyātu iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum - kuyiṉeṉṉuñ col tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	kuyiṉkuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟivu eṉa varum. kuyiṉeṉpatu mēkam. aḵtu aḵṟiṇip peyar. 
toki marapiṉuḷ uyartiṇip peyarum viravup peyarum iyalpāka veṉṟār. 
31kuyiṉ viṉiyumām.
iyaṟki eṉṟataṉāṟ, kāṉkōḻi kōṉkuṇam vāṉ kari eṉa varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)
336.		ekiṉmara māyi ṉāṇmara viyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ekiṉ maramāyiṉ - ekiṉeṉpatu puḷḷaṉṟi marap peyarāyiṉ, āṇmara iyaṟṟu - āṇmarattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  ekiṉaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.				(41)
337.		ēṉi yekiṉē yakaram varumē
					valleḻut tiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum.
ituvum atu, tiripu vilakki akaram vitittaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi ekiṉē akaram varum - maramallāta ekiṉ nili moḻikkaṇ akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, valleḻuttiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum - āṇṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttiyalpu mikku muṭitali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ekiṉakkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattōṭu itaṉi oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi yāppu aṭivu eṉa varum.
iyaṟki yeṉṟataṉāṉ, akarattōṭu melleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka. ekiṉaṅkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
iṉic ciṟupāṉmi ekiṉcēval ekiṉaccēval peṭi eṉpaṉa āṟaṉurupu virivuḻi īṇṭi ilēcāṉ muṭikka. 32paṇpu karutiya vaḻi ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka.	(42)
338.		kiḷippeya rellāṅ kiḷippeya riyala.
itu, ṉakaran tiritali vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷip peyarellām - ṉakara īṟṟuk kiḷippeya rellām, kiḷippeyar iyala - ṇakara īṟṟuk kiḷippeyar pōlat tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  eyiṉkuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum. eyiṉ vantatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇikkum eytutaliṉ, toki marapiṉuḷ muṭiyātāyiṟṟu, āṇṭu uyartiṇikkē kūṟutaliṉ.
iṉi ellā meṉṟataṉāṉē, eyiṉakkaṉṉi piḷḷi eṉa akkum valleḻuttum peṟutalum, eyiṉa vāḻvu eṉa valle ḻuttup peṟāmiyum koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pārppaṉak kaṉṉi kumari cēri piḷḷi eṉa ākāram kuṟukki akkum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum, pārppaṉa vāḻkki eṉa valleḻuttuk koṭātum muṭikka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē nāṉku kaṇattuk kaṇṇum veḷḷāḷaṉeṉa niṉṟataṉi aṉkeṭuttup pirittu ḷakāra voṟṟiṉi ṇakāra voṟṟākki, veḷḷāṇ kumari piḷḷi māntar vāḻkki oḻukkam eṉa muṭikka.
 iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, mutaleḻutti nīṭṭi ḷakāra voṟṟiṉik keṭuttu vēḷāṇeṉa muṭikka. 
itaṉāṉē, poruna vāḻkkiyum muṭikka. 33vēṭṭuvak kumari eṉpatu marūu vaḻakku.	(43)
339.		mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
ituvum atu, taṉ tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - mīṉeṉṉuñ col tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: mīṉkaṇ mīṟkaṇ, mīṉciṉi mīṟciṉi, mīṉṟali mīṟṟali, mīṉpuṟam mīṟpuṟam eṉa varum.	(44)
340.		tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
					mēṉili yottalum valleḻuttu mikutalu
					māmuṟi yiraṇṭu murimiyu muṭittē
					valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yilli.
ituvum atu, mēlataṉoṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ:tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - tē ṉeṉṉuñ col valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy variṉ, mēlnili ottalum - mīṉeṉpataṟkuk kūṟiya tiri puṟaḻcci nili ottu muṭitalum, valleḻuttu mikutalum - varu moḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumākiya, āmuṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu - ammuṟimi yiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭaṉiyum urittātaliyum uṭittu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟutiyilli - valleḻuttu mikku varumiṭattu iṟutiyil niṉṟa ṉakaraṅ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
urimiyu meṉṉum ummi, melleḻuttu mikiṉum (eḻu. 341) eṉa mēlvarukiṉṟataṉi nōkkiṟṟu.
utāraṇam:  tēṉkuṭam tēṟkuṭam, cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa mēṉili ottaṉa. tēkkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.	(45)
341.		melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.
ituvum atu; uṟaḻcciyum valleḻuttum vilakki melleḻuttum vitittaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli - muṟkūṟiya tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu vantāl avval leḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.
ṉakarakkēṭu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
utāraṇam: tēṅkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum.	(46)
342.		melleḻut tiyiyi ṉiṟutiyo ṭuṟaḻum.
itu, toki marapiṉuḷ vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum valle ḻuttal vaḻi (eḻu. 148) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya iyalpi vilakki uṟaḻumeṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - attēṉeṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ, iṟutiyoṭu uṟaḻum - nili moḻi yiṟutiyiṉ ṉakara voṟṟuk keṭutaluṅ keṭāmiyumākiya uṟaḻcci yāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: tēṉñeri tēñeri, tēṉuṉi tēnuṉi, tēṉmoḻi tēmoḻi eṉa varum.
mēl āmuṟi (eḻu.. 340) eṉṟataṉāl, tēñeri tēññeri, tēnuṉi, tēnnuṉi, tēmoḻi tēmmoḻi eṉa ṉakaraṅ keṭṭut tattam melleḻuttu mikkum mikātum muṭintaṉavuṅ koḷka.
iṉi 34mēl māṉamilli (eḻu. 341) eṉṟataṉāṉ, īṟu keṭṭu melleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavuṅ koṇṭu tēñeri tēnuṉi tēmoḻi eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avi muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅku meṉka.	(47)
343.		iṟāaṟ tōṟṟa miyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu attēṉeṉpataṟku uyirkkaṇattu orumoḻi muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: iṟāaṟ ṟōṟṟam - tēṉeṉṉuñ col iṟā leṉṉum varumoḻiyatu tōṟṟattukkaṇ, iyaṟki yākum - nilimoḻiyiṉ ṉakaraṅ keṭātē niṉṟu iyalpāy  muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.	(48)
utāraṇam:  tēṉiṟāl eṉa varum.
344.		oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalu murittē.
ituvum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittu - attē ṉeṉpatu iṟāloṭu puṇarumiṭattup piṟitumōr takarattōṭu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟutiyilli (eḻut. 340) eṉṟata ṉāṉ, nilimoḻi īṟu keṭukka. takarammiku meṉṉātu oṟṟumiku takara meṉṟataṉāṉ, īroṟṟākkuka.
utāraṇam:  tēttiṟāl eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattōṭu itaṉi yoṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṟ, piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum immuṭipu koḷka. tēttaṭi, tēttī eṉa varum.
tōṟṟa meṉṟataṉāṉ, tēṉaṭi, tēṉī eṉṉum iyalpuṅ koḷka.		(49)
345.		miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉu
					mannāṟ collun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintum varuka eṉa eytuvitta muṭipi vilakkit toḻiṟ peyari yalpāmeṉap piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum annāṟ collum - miṉṉeṉṉuñ collum piṉṉeṉṉuñ collum paṉṉeṉṉuñ collum kaṉṉeṉṉuñ collumākiya annāṉku collum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉuṅ ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉ kaṇattum iṭik kaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: miṉṉuccey viḷakkattu (kali. 41: 6) piṉṉup piṇi yaviḻnta  eṉavum, paṉṉukkaṭitu kaṉṉukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, miṉṉukkaṭumi piṉṉuk kaṭumi paṉṉuk kaṭumi kaṉṉuk kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.
toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala eṉṟu ōtātu kiḷantōtiṉār, ivi toḻiṉilikkaṇṇaṉṟi vēṟu tam poruḷuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum immuṭipu eytu meṉṟaṟku.
35miṉ ṉeṉpatu miṉṉutaṟṟoḻilum, miṉṉunimirn taṉṉa (puṟam. 57) eṉa miṉṉeṉappaṭuvatōr poruḷum uṇarttum. ēṉiya vum aṉṉa.	(50)
346.		vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yekiṉoṭu
					tōṟṟa mokkuṅ kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.
itu, nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ ēṉi ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam okkum - vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ oḻinta maramallāta ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam otta akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum, kaṉ eṉ kiḷavi - kaṉṉeṉṉuñcol eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kaṉṉakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉa varum. kaṉṉakkaṭumi eṉak kuṇa vēṟṟumi yuñ ciṟupāṉmi koḷka.
tōṟṟa meṉṟataṉāṉ, alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu akaramum melleḻuttum ēṉikkaṇattu akaramuṅ koḷka. kaṉṉaṅkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, kaṉṉañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum. kaṉṉaṅkaṭumi eṉak 36kuṇa vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka.
poṉṉakar varippiṟ kaṉṉan tūkki (aiṅkuṟu. 247) eṉpatō veṉiṉ, atu makara īṟṟup poruṭ peyar.	(51)
347.		iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti muṟivariṉ
					mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa vakara niliyu
					meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu mavviyaṟ peyarē.
iḵtu aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē (eḻu. 155) eṉṟataṟku īṇṭut tiripu kūṟaliṉ, eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti 
muṟivariṉ - iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyariṉ muṉṉart tanti yeṉṉum muṟippeyar varumoḻiyāy varumāyiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram niliyum - attanti yeṉpataṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa takaravoṟ ṟukkeṭa ataṉmēlēṟi niṉṟa akaraṅ keṭātu niṟkum, avviyaṟ peyar meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭum - annilimoḻiyākiya iyaṟpeyar aṉ ṉeṉṉuñ colliṉ akaram ēṟiniṉṟa mey yi oḻittu avvaṉtāṉ keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: cāttanti, koṟṟanti eṉa varum.
mutaṟkaṇmey yeṉṟataṉāl, cāttaṉṟanti koṟṟaṉ ṟanti eṉṉum iyalpu muṭipuṅ koḷka.	(52)
348.		ātaṉum pūtaṉuṅ kūṟiya viyalpoṭu
					peyaroṟ ṟakaran tuvarak keṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ātaṉum pūtaṉum - muṟkūṟiya iyaṟ peyaruḷ ātaṉum pūtaṉum eṉṉum iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tanti yeṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu muṭiyuṅkāl, kūṟiya iyal
poṭu - muṟ kūṟiya nilimoḻi aṉ keṭutalum varumoḻit takara voṟṟuk keṭutalumākiya ceykikaḷuṭaṉē, peyaroṟṟu akaran tuvarak keṭum - nilimoḻip peyaril aṉkeṭa niṉṟa takaravoṟṟum varumoḻiyil takara voṟṟukkeṭa niṉṟa akaramum muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ānti, pūnti eṉa varum. iyalpu eṉṟataṉāṟ, peyaroṟṟum akaramum keṭātē niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. ātanti pūtanti eṉa varum.
iṉit tuvara veṉṟataṉāl, aḻāṉ puḻāṉ eṉa niṟuttit tanti eṉa varuvittu, nilimoḻi ṉakaramum varumoḻit takara mum akaramuṅ keṭuttu, aḻānti puḻānti eṉa muṭikka.	(53)
349.		ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi yiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi - avviyaṟ peyar paṇpu aṭuttu varumvaḻi, iyaṟkiyākum - muṟkūṟiya iruvakic ceyki yum tavirttu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:peruñcāttaṉṟanti, peruṅkoṟ ṟaṉṟanti eṉa varum. koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉṟanti, cāttaṅ koṟṟaṉṟanti eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa paṇpaṉṟi aṭi aṭuttaṉavātaliṟ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka.(54)
350.		appeyar meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu vaḻiyu
					niṟṟalu murittē yameṉ cāriyi
					makkaṇ muṟito kūu maruṅki ṉāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku vēṟōr varumoḻikkaṇ eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: appeyar makkaḷ āṉa muṟi tokūum maruṅ kiṉum - avviyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantiyaṉṟi makaṉākiya muṟip peyar vantu tokumiṭattiṉum, meyyoḻittu aṉ keṭuvaḻi ammeṉ cāriyi niṟṟalum urittu - avviyaṟ peyariṉ tāṉ ēṟiya mey niṟka aṉ keṭṭu ammuccāriyi vantu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
āṉa eṉpataṉi makkaḷōṭum, ummiyi maruṅki ṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. muṟi tokūu maruṅki ṉeṉṟatu, iṉṉāṟku makaṉeṉṉum muṟippeyarāyc cērumiṭat teṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ eṉa nili moḻi aṉ keṭṭuḻi ammu vantatu. ivaṟṟiṟku, atu veṉurupu viriyātu ataṉ uṭimip poruḷ virikka. itu muṟippeyar.
iṉi ummiyāṟ, 37koṟṟaṅkuṭi cāttaṅkuṭi eṉap piṟa peyar tokkaṉavuṅ koḷka. 
meyyoḻit teṉṟataṉāṉē, koṟṟamaṅkalam cātta maṅkalam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ ammiṉ makaraṅ keṭutalum, vēṭṭa maṅkalam, vēṭṭaṉkuṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ nilimoḻi yoṟṟu iraṭṭutaluṅ koḷka. (55)
351.		tāṉum pēṉuṅ kōṉu meṉṉu
					māmuṟi yiyaṟpeyar tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum ā muṟi iyaṟ peyar - avviyaṟpeyaruḷ tāṉum pēṉuṅ kōṉumeṉṉum ammuṟi yiṉi yuṭiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ, tantiyoṭum makkaḷ muṟimi yoṭum puṇarum vaḻi, tiripiṭaṉila - muṟkūṟiya tiripukaḷiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu..
utāraṇam:  tāṉṟanti pēṉṟanti kōṉṟanti eṉavum, tāṉkoṟṟaṉ pēṉkoṟṟaṉ, kōṉkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum varum. pēṉ kōṉ eṉpaṉa muṟkālattu vaḻakku. ivi toki marapiṉuḷ aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar (eḻu. 155) eṉpuḻi iyalpāyiṉavēṉum īṇṭu ivvīṟṟiṟkut tiripu kūṟutaliṉ, . vilakki iyalpāmeṉpa tūuṅ kūṟiṉār.	(56)
352.		tāṉyā ṉeṉumpeya rurupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu, toki marapiṉuḷ aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar (eḻu. 155) eṉpataṉāṉē iyalpāy niṉṟa tāṉ eṉpataṉiyum, uyirīṟākiya (eḻu. 153) eṉpataṉāl iyalpāy niṉṟa yā ṉeṉpataṉiyum avviyalpu vilakki urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉ yāṉ eṉum peyar - tāṉeṉṉum viravup peyarum yāṉeṉṉum uyartiṇip peyarum, urupiyal niliyum - urupiyaliṟ kūṟiya iyalpilē nilipeṟṟut tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukit taṉeṉṟum, yāṉeṉpatu ākāram ekaramāy yakaraṅ keṭṭu eṉ eṉṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.. 
utāraṇam: taṉki, eṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattuk kaṇṇun taṉñāṇ eṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa varum.	(57)

353.		vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kuṟukalun tiritalun
					tōṟṟa milli yeṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu - alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytu vittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - muṟkūṟiya tāṉ yāṉ eṉpaṉa vēṟṟumip puṇarcci yallātaviṭattu, kuṟukalun tiritalun tōṟṟamilli eṉmaṉār pulavar - tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukutalum yāṉeṉpatu avvāṟu tiritalun tōṟṟamiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: tāṉkuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇ ṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum; yāṉ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ ñāṉṟēṉ nīṇṭēṉ māṇṭēṉ valiyēṉ eṉavum varum.
tōṟṟa meṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇ avvāṟaṉṟi ṉakaram tiritaluṅ koḷka. 38taṟpukaḻ taṟpāṭi, eṟpukaḻ eṟpāṭi eṉa varum.	(58)
354.	aḻaṉe ṉiṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu, vēṟṟumikkaṇ akaran tiriyātu keṭuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḻaṉ eṉ iṟutikeṭa - aḻaṉeṉṉuñ col taṉ īṟṟu ṉakaraṅ keṭa, valleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	aḻakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum. aḻakkuṭa meṉpatu piṇakkuṭatti.	(59)
355.		muṉṉeṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟu
					milleṉ kiḷavimici ṟakara moṟṟa
					ṟolliyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapē.
itu, maruviṉṟokuti (eḻu. 111) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya ilakkaṇa marūukkaḷiṉ oṉṟaṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ eṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum il eṉ kiḷavi mici - muṉṉeṉṉuñ colliṉ muṉṉē varum il leṉṉuñ colliṉ mēlē, ṟakaram oṟṟal - oru ṟakara voṟṟu vantu niṉṟu muṭital, tolliyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu - paḻiyatākiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya vaḻakkiṭattu maruvi vanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: muṉṟil eṉa varum. ilmuṉ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu, muṉṟil eṉṟu talitaṭu māṟutaliṉ marūuvāyiṟṟu. muṉṉeṉpataṟku 39oṟṟiraṭṭutal ilakkaṇamēṉum, atuvaṉṟit taṉakku iṉamāyatoru ṟakara voṟṟup peṟutaliṉ vēṟu muṭipāyiṟṟu.	(60)
356.		poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yīṟukeṭa muṟiyiṉ	
					muṉṉart tōṉṟum lakāra makārañ
					ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṟ ṟoṭariya lāṉa.
iḵtu avvīṟṟup peyaroṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: poṉ eṉ kiḷavi īṟu keṭa -  poṉ eṉṉuñ col taṉ īṟākiya ṉakaraṅ keṭāniṟka, muṉṉar lakāra makāram muṟiyil tōṉṟum - ataṉmuṉṉar lakaramum makaravoṟṟum muṟiyāṉē vantu niṟkum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toṭariyalāṉa - aṅṅaṉam niṟpatu ceyyuḷiṭattuc coṟkaḷ tammuḷ toṭarccip paṭum iyalpiṉkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
muṟiyi ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ, makaram oṟṟātal koḷka.
utāraṇam: polampaṭip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi (malipaṭu. 574) eṉa varum.
toṭariyalāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉē, vaṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum lakaram niṟka, makaram valleḻuttiṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tirital koḷka. polaṅkalañ cumanta pūṇṭāṅ kiḷamuli (akam. 19), polañcuṭa rāḻi pūṇṭa tērē, polantārk kuṭṭuvaṉ  eṉa varum. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pola naṟunteriyal (puṟam. 29) polamala rāviri eṉṟāṟ pōla makaraṅ keṭṭup piṟakaṇattu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.	(61)
357.		yakara viṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ
					valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē yakara īṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ - yakara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi vantu iyiyiṉ avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nāykkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(62)
358.		tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.
itu, viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tāyeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum - tāyeṉṉuñ col valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu..
utāraṇam: tāyki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattāṉ mikutiyuṅ kūṟutaliṉ, aḵṟiṇi viravup peyaruḷ (155) aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu.	(63)
359.		makaṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ mutaṉili yiyaṟṟē.
iḵtu - eytāta teytuvittatu; tāyeṉpatu aṭi yaṭuttuḻi valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: makaṉ viṉi kiḷappiṉ - tāyeṉṉuñ col taṉakku aṭiyāy muṉvanta makaṉatu viṉiyip piṉṉāka oruvaṉ kūṟumiṭattu, mutal nili iyaṟṟu - ivvīṟṟuḷ mutaṟkaṭ kūṟiya nilimiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	makaṉṟāykkalām ceru tuṟattal pakittal eṉa varum. makaṉ tāyōṭu kalāytta kalām eṉa viriyum. ēṉiya vaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkum urupu virikka. Éid, <©L¥ gifnk‰W.(64)
360.		melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
iḵtu - eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa - yakara īṟṟuḷ atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttu mikku uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	vēykkuṟi vēyṅkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavarum.	(65)
361.	alvaḻi yellā miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu - avvīṟṟu alvaḻikku eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻi ellām iyalpeṉa moḻipa - yakara īṟṟu alvaḻi ellām iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nāykaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.
ellā meṉṟataṉāṉ, avvāykkoṇṭāṉ ivvāyk koṇṭāṉ uvvāykkoṇṭāṉ evvāyk koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa muṭi  vaṉavum, tāyk koṇṭāṉ tūyppeytāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlum viṉiyeccamum, poyccol meyccol eyppaṉṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum paṇput tokiyum, vēykkaṭitu vēykaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci muṭivuṅ koḷka.	(66)
362.		rakāra viṟuti yakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē rakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: rakāra iṟuti - rakāra īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, yakāra iyaṟṟu-yakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: tērkkāl celavu tali puṟam eṉa varum.
immāṭṭēṟṟiṉi, yakara īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻi yeṉṉum iraṇṭiyum karuti māṭṭeṟintāreṉpar, alvaḻi muṭipum īṇṭuk kāṭṭuvar. yām ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṟ kāṭṭutum. 
itu ḻakara īṟṟiṟkum okkum.

363.		ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum
					melleḻuttu mikutaṉ meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum - āreṉṉuñ collum vetireṉṉuñ collum cāreṉṉuñ collum pīreṉṉuñ collum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟat tōṉṟum - melleḻuttu mikku muṭital meymmi peṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ārṅkōṭu, vetirṅkōṭu, cārṅkōṭu, pīrṅ koṭi, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
40pīr marameṉpār pīrṅkōṭeṉpar. pīrvāyp pirintanīr niṟi muṟi ceytu eṉṟāṟpōlac cāṉṟōr palaruñ ceyyuḷ ceytavāṟu kāṇka.

364.		cāreṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum.

365.		pīreṉ kiḷavi yammoṭuñ civaṇum.
