366.		lakāra viṟuti ṉakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē lakāra īṟṟi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṭ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟu - lakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṉakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy lakaram ṟakaramāyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kaṟkuṟi ciṟi tali puṟam, neṟkatir cōṟu tali puṟam eṉavarum.	(71)
367.		melleḻut tiyiyi ṉakāra mākum.
itu, ṉakāramā meṉṟaliṉ ataṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṉakāra mākum - avvīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyiyiṉ ṉakaramākat tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kaṉñeri nuṉi muri eṉa varum.
iccūttirattiṉi vēṟṟumi yiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttuk kōṭaṟkaṇ ciṅkanōkkāka vittamiyāṉ, alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka.  kaṉñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum.	(72)
368.		alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.

utāraṇam:  kalkuṟitu kaṟkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. 
ellā meṉṟataṉāṟ, kalkuṟumi kaṟkuṟumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉak kuṇampaṟṟivanta vēṟṟumikkum uṟaḻcci koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē viṉiccollīṟu tirintaṉavum urupu tirintaṉavuṅ koḷka. vantāṉāṟkoṟṟaṉ poruvāṉāṟ pōkāṉ eṉavum 42attāṟkoṇṭāṉ ittāṟkoṇ ṭāṉ uttāṟ koṇṭāṉ ettāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum.
43akkāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlap piṟavum muṭipu uḷḷaṉa vellām itaṉāṉ muṭittuk koḷka.	(73)
369.		takaram varuvaḻi yāyta niliyalum
					pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē.
itu lakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamākavum tiriyu meṉṟa liṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: takaram varuvaḻi āytam niliyalum - takaram mutalākiya moḻi vantāl lakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamākat tirintu niṟṟalum, pukar iṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulamiyōr - kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉṟu colluvār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu kaṟṟītu eṉa varum.
pukariṉṟeṉṟataṉāl, neṭiyataṉiṟuti (eḻu. 370) eṉpa taṉuḷ vēṟītu vēṟṟītu eṉṉum uṟaḻcci muṭipuṅ koḷka.	(74)
370.		neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpumā ruḷavē.
iḵtu alvaḻiyellāmuṟaḻ (eḻu. 368) eṉṟataṉi vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumāruḷa - neṭṭeḻuttiṉ īṟṟu lakāra īṟu kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakāram pōlat tirintu uṟaḻtalē yaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: pālkaṭitu  tītu peritu  eṉavarum. iyalpākātu tirintaṉa vēṟkaṭitu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa.	(75)
371.		nelluñ celluṅ kolluñ collu
					mallatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi yiyala.
iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ uṟaḻntu muṭika eṉṟataṉi vēṟṟumi muṭi peṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nelluñ celluṅ kolluñ collum - nelleṉṉuñ collum celleṉṉuñ collum kolleṉṉuñ collum colleṉṉuñ collumākiya in nāṉku collum, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi iyala - al vaḻiyic collumiṭattun tām vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṉa vāy lakaram ṟakaramāyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
ummi ciṟappu.
utāraṇam: neṟkāyttatu. ceṟkaṭitu, koṟkaṭitu, coṟkaṭitu; ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(76)
372.		illeṉ kiḷavi yiṉmi ceppiṉ
					valleḻuttu mikutalu miyiṭi varutalu
					miyaṟki yātalu mākāram varutaluṅ
					koḷattaku marapi ṉākiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟu viṉikkuṟippuc colluḷ oṉṟaṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppiṉ - illeṉṉuñ col iruppiṭamākiya illi uṇarttātu oru poruḷiṉatu illāmiyi uṇarttum iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum - valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, ai iṭi varutalum - aikāram iṭiyē varutalum, iyaṟkiyātalum - iraṇṭum vārātu iyalpāy muṭitalum, ākāram varutalum - ākāram vantu muṭitalumākiya in nāṉku muṭipum, koḷattaku marapiṉ - coṟkumuṭipākak koḷattakum muṟiyāṉē, ākiṭaṉuṭittu - taṉ muṭipām iṭaṉ uṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
koḷattaku marapi ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻi aikāram varutalum, aikāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum mikāmiyum, ākāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam: 	ileṉa niṟuttik, koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉat tantu valleḻuttum aikāramuṅ koṭuttu illik koṟṟaṉeṉa ēṉiyavaṟ ṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
iṉṉum avvāṟē niṟutti, aikāramē koṭuttu illikoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉa valleḻuttu mikātu muṭikka.
iṉṉum koḷattaku marapi ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ, ēṉik kaṇattiṉ muṉṉum aikāramē koṭuttu illi ñāṇ nūl maṇi vāṉam āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
iḵtu, ileṉpatoru mutaṉili niṉṟu varumoḻi yōṭu iṅṅaṉam puṇarntateṉpatu uṇartaṟku illeṉ kiḷavi eṉṟum, iyaṟkiyātalum eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. immuṭipu viṉi yiyaluḷ viravu viṉikkaṇ iṉmiceppal eṉpuḻi illi il (col. 222) eṉṟu uri kūṟiyavata ṉāṉum, avaṉilli eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa utāraṇamāka ellā āciriyaruṅ kāṭṭiya vāṟṟāṉum uṇarka.
itaṉāṉē, iṅṅaṉam puṇartta collaṉṟi illi eṉa aikāra īṟāy niṟpatoru col iṉmiyum uṇarka. āyiṉ, iṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ivvāṟē puṇarkka eṉiṉ, avi varumoḻiyiṉṟi oru collāy niṟṟaliṉ puṇarkkārāyiṉār.
iṉi, iyalpu varumāṟu:- eṇṇilkuṇam ceyki tuṭi poruḷ eṉavum, poyyilñāṉam, miyilvāṇmukam (kali.7) eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ākāram varumāṟu:-illākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ poruḷ eṉa ākāram valleḻuttup peṟṟu varum.
44piṟkūṟiya iraṇṭum illeṉṉum viṉikkuṟippu mutaṉili yaṭiyākat tōṉṟiya peyarecca maṟi tokkum virintum niṉṟaṉa.
iyalpu muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, immuṭipiṟku 45vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceyka. tāviṉīṭci eṉṟāṟpōl vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceytu muṭikka.	(77)
373.		valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu  iruvaḻiyun tirintum uṟaḻntum varumeṉa eytiya taṉi vilakkit toḻiṟpeyarōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṟ piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: val eṉ kiḷavi - valleṉṉuñ col alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum val leḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭik kaṇattu vakārattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: vallukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.	(78)
374.		nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
					yāvayi ṉukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
					yukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi yakara niliyum.
iḵtu eytiyatu oruvaḻi vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli - valleṉpataṉ muṉ nāyeṉṉuñ collum palakiyeṉṉuñ collum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āvayiṉ ukaraṅ keṭutalum urittu - avviṭattu ukaram keṭātu niṟṟalē yaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum, ukaraṅkeṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum - avvukaraṅ keṭu miṭattu akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: valla nāy, vallappalaki eṉa varum.
ummi etirmaṟiyākalāṉ ukaraṅ keṭātē niṉṟu, vallunāy valluppalaki eṉa varutaluṅ koḷka. 
akaram nili yum eṉṉātu ukaraṅ keṭum eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa varumoḻik kaṇṇum ivvakarappēṟu koḷka.  vallak kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa varum.	(79)
375.		pūlvē leṉṟā vāleṉ kiḷaviyo
					ṭāmup peyarkku mammiṭi varumē.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: pūl vēl eṉṟā āleṉ kiḷaviyōṭu āmup peyarkkum - pūleṉṉuñ collum vēleṉṉuñ collum āleṉṉuñ collumākiya ammūṉṟu peyarkkum, am iṭi varum - vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripiṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  pūlaṅkōṭu, vēlaṅkōṭu, ālaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattum oṭṭuka.  	 pūlañeri, vēlañeri, ālañeri, nīḻal viṟaku eṉavarum.
eṉṟā eṉa eṇṇiṭi yiṭṭamiyāṟ, 46pūlāṅkōṭu pūlāṅkaḻi eṉa ākāram pūlukkukkoḷka.	(80)
376.		toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇun tiripum uṟaḻcciyum vilakki ñakārīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām - lakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyariṉ iyalpiṉavāy iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: pullukkaṭitu, kallukkaṭitu, vallukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟkup pullutal, kallutal, vallutal eṉap poru ḷurikka,
iṉi, ellā meṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarviti eytātu piṟaviti eytuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. kaṉṉalkaṭitu, piṉṉalkaṭitu, kaṉṉaṟkaṭumi, piṉṉaṟ kaṭumi eṉavum varum. itaṉāṉē, meṉkaṇam vantuḻip piṉṉaṉ ñāṉṟatu nīṇ ṭatu māṇṭatu, piṉṉaṉ ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉa oṭṭuka. iṉi, āṭal pāṭal kūṭal nīṭal mutaliyaṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintum muṭital itaṉāṟ koḷka.	(81)
377.		veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiya ṉiliyum.
itu, tiripuvilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: veyil eṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal 
niliyum - veyileṉṉuñ col maḻiyeṉṉuñ coṟ pōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
maḻi yeṉpataṉi vaḷiyeṉa varūum (eḻu. 242) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum, vaḷiyeṉpataṉip paṉiyeṉavarūum (eḻu.241) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum māṭṭeṟintavāṟu kāṇka.
utāraṇam: veyilattukkoṇṭāṉ veyiliṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. iḵtu, attumici yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṉṟa iṭam. iḵtu, avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu (eḻu. 133) mikkatu, atikāra valleḻuttiṉmiyiṉ. iyalpu kaṇattuṅ koḷka, 47cāriyi varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ. 	(82)
378.		cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara viṟuti
					muṟpaṭak kiḷanta vurupiya ṉiliyum.
itu, muṟiyāṉē vakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakara iṟuti - vakara īṟṟup peyar nāṉkaṉuḷ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakara īṟṟup peyar mūṉṟum, muṟpaṭak kiḷanta urupiyal niliyum - muṟpaṭak kūṟiya urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: avaṟṟukkōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟuk kōṭu, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
muṟpaṭak kiḷanta eṉṟataṉāṉē, vaṟṟiṉōṭu iṉṉum peṟutal koḷka. avaṟṟiṉ kōṭu ivaṟṟiṉ kōṭu uvaṟṟiṉ kōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu ēṉik kaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka.			(83)
379.		vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi āytamākum - accuṭṭumutal vakaram vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ vēṟṟumi yallāta iṭattu āytamāyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	aḵkaṭiya iḵkaṭiya uḵkaṭiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa varum. ivvaḻakku ikkālattu aritu.	(84)
380.		melleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻut tākum.
iḵtu  eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻut tākum - avvakara īṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyiyumāyiṉ avvakara voṟṟu avvam melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: aññāṇ iññāṇ uññāṇ, nūl maṇi eṉavarum.	(85)
381.		ēṉavi puṇari ṉiyalpeṉa moḻipa.
ituvum atu, avvīṟu ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ:ēṉavi puṇariṉ-accuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟōṭu iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇarumāyiṉ, iyalpeṉa moḻipa - avvakaran tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyu meṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: avyāḻ ivyāḻ uvyāḻ, vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu nilimoḻikkeṉṟum, āṇṭu niṉṟa coṉmuṉiyalpākum (eḻut. 144) eṉṟatu varumoḻik keṉṟum uṇarka.	(86)
382.		ēṉi vakaran toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu  eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi vakaram - vakarakkiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu (eḻut. 81) eṉṟataṉuḷ oḻintu niṉṟa uriccollākiya vakaram iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	48tevvukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tevvukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
uriyiṟ kōṭa leṉpataṉāṉ, temmuṉi eṉa vakara voṟṟu makara voṟṟākat tirital koḷka.	(87)
383.		ḻakāra viṟuti rakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ḻakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟu - ḻakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumikkaṇ rakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: pūḻkkāl, ciṟaku tali puṟam eṉa  varum.		(88)
384.		tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇari
					ṉakkiṭi varuta lurittu mākum.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu akku vakuttaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: tāḻ eṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ - tāḻeṉṉuñ col kōleṉṉuñ collōṭu puṇarum iṭattu, akku iṭi varutalum urittākum - valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyi iṭiyē vantu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, akkuppeṟātu valleḻuttu mikutal valiyuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
utāraṇam: tāḻakkōl, tāḻkkōl eṉa varum.
itu tāḻit tiṟakkuṅkōl eṉa viriyum.	(89)
385.		tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tamiḻ eṉ kiḷaviyum - tamiḻeṉṉuñ collum, ataṉōraṟṟu - valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalēyaṉṟi akkuc cāriyi peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
ataṉōraṟṟē eṉṟataṉāl, itaṟkut tamiḻk kūtteṉa valleḻuttu mikutalē valiyuṭittu.
utāraṇam: tamiḻakkūttu, cēri tōṭṭam paḷḷi eṉa varum. tamiḻiyuṭiya kūttu eṉa virikka. tamiḻ variyar eṉṟāṟpōla, valleḻuttup peṟātu akkup peṟṟaṉa, uṇarakkūṟiya (eḻu. 405) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭi yāṟ koḷka. tamiḻanāṭu tamiḻnāṭu eṉa ēṉik kaṇattu muṭipu, eppeyar muṉṉarum (eḻu. 128) eṉpataṉuḷ, muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittām.	(90)
386.		kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṟ
					pīreṉ kiḷaviyō ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.
itu, valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttum ammum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kumiḻ eṉ kiḷavi marappeyar āyiṉ - kumilēṉṉum col kumiḻttal eṉṉun toḻilaṉṟi marappeyarāyiṉ, pīr eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ōr iyaṟṟu ākum - pīr eṉṉum collōṭu ōriyal piṟṟāy oruvaḻi melleḻuttum oruvaḻi ammum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	kumiḻṅkōṭu, kumiḻaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
ōriyaṟṟu ṟeṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaṟṟiṟkum immuṭipu koḷka. makiḻṅkōṭu, makiḻaṅkōṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(91)
387.		pāḻeṉ kiḷavi melleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
itu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttup peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: pāḻ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - pāḻeṉṉuñ collīṟu valleḻuttiṉōṭu melle ḻuttup peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	pāḻkkiṇaṟu pāḻṅkiṇaṟu cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa oṭṭuka. itu pāḻuṭkiṇaṟu eṉa viriyum, pāḻttakiṇaṟu eṉa viṉit toki muṭiyāmiyiṉ.	(92)
388.		ēḻeṉ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu - eṇṇuppeyar ivvāṟu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ, eytā tatu eytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ēḻ eṉ kiḷavi - ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar iṟuti, urupiyal niliyum - urupu puṇarccikkaṇ kūṟiya iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē nilipeṟṟu aṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
aḵtu aṉṉeṉ cāriyi yēḻa ṉiṟuti (eḻu. 194) eṉpatām.
utāraṇam: :	ēḻaṉkāyam, cukku tōri payaṟu eṉa varum. iyipu valleḻuttu ōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ vīḻkka. iḵtu ēḻaṉāṟkoṇṭa kāyam eṉa viriyum.	(93)
389.		aḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇum varuvaḻi
					neṭumutal kuṟukalu mukaram varutaluṅ
					kaṭinili yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka.
itu, mēlataṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum varuvaḻi - avvē ḻeṉpataṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇup peyarum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutaluṅ kaṭiniliyiṉṟē āciriyaṟ - muṉṉiṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ māttiri kuṟukutalum āṇṭu ukaram varutalum nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyaṟku eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: eḻukalam, cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum; eḻukaḻañcu, toṭi palam eṉavum, eḻumūṉṟu eḻunāṉku eṉavum varum.
nili yeṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇattup poruṭpeyarkkum immuṭipu koḷka. eḻukaṭal, cili tici piṟappu eṉavarum. ‘(94)
390.		patteṉ kiḷavi yoṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi
					niṟṟal vēṇṭu māytap puḷḷi.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, varumoḻi nōkki vitittaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: pattu eṉ kiḷavi oṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi - avvēḻa ṉōṭu patteṉpatu puṇarumiṭattu appatteṉ kiḷavi iṭi yoṟṟuk keṭuvaḻi, āytappuḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - āytamākiya puḷḷi niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: eḻupaḵtu eṉa varum.	(95)
391.		āyiram varuvaḻi yukaraṅ keṭumē.
itu, neṭumutal kuṟuki niṉṟu ukaram peṟātu eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram varuvaḻi-ēḻeṉpataṉ muṉ āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, ukaraṅ keṭum-neṭu mutal kuṟukiniṉṟu ukarampeṟātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:  eḻāyiram eṉa varum.	(96)
392.		nūṟūrntu varūu māyirak kiḷavikkuk
					kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.
iḵtu - eytiyatu muḻuvatūum vilakkiṟṟu, ukaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉ mēlē neṭumutal kuṟukātu eṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟu ūrntu varūum āyiram kiḷavikku - avvē ḻeṉpatu nūṟeṉṉuñ colmēl varum āyira meṉṉuñ colliṟku, kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukkamiṉṟu - muṟkūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutaliṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ēḻ nūṟāyiram eṉavarum.
kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki 49ukaram peṟṟu eḻu nūṟāyiram eṉavum varum. 
itaṉāṉē, ēḻāyirameṉa mēl mutaṉili kuṟukāmi yuṅ koḷka. 
itaṉāṉē, eḻuñāyiṟu eḻu nāḷ eḻuvaki eṉa iyalpu kaṇattu neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutalum koḷka.	(97)
393.		aiyam palleṉa varūu miṟuti
					yalpeya reṇṇiṉu māyiya ṉiliyum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: 50ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti - avvēḻaṉ muṉṉar aiyeṉṟum ammeṉṟum palleṉṟum varukiṉṟa iṟutikaḷi yuṭiya, alpeyar eṇṇiṉum - poruṭ peyarallāta eṇṇup peyarākiya tāmari veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉa vantālum, ā iyal niliyum - neṭumutal kuṟukātu ukaram peṟṟātu avviyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ēḻtāmari, ēḻveḷḷam, ēḻāmpal eṉa varum.	(98)
394.		uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmuṉ variṉum - avvēḻeṉpataṉ muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum eṇṇup peyarumākiya uyir mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ā iyal tiriyātu - neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟṟu vārātu muṭiyum iyalpiṟ ṟiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ēḻakal, ēḻuḻakku, ēḻoṉṟu, ēḻiraṇṭu eṉa  varum.	(99)
395.		kīḻeṉ kiḷavi yuṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu  ivvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ uṟaḻcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:kīḻ eṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻat tōṉṟum - kīḻeṉṉuñ col uṟaḻcciyāyt tōṉṟi muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
tōṉṟu meṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātu valle ḻuttup peṟṟum peṟātum varumeṉṟa iraṇṭum uṟaḻcciyāy varumeṉṟu koḷka. iyipu valleḻuttu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
utāraṇam: kīḻkkuḷam kīḻkuḷam, cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum.			(100)
396.		ḷakāra viṟuti ṇakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ḷakāra īṟṟuccol vēṟṟumik kaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟu - ḷakāra īṟṟup peyar ṇakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṭakāra māyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: muṭkuṟi, ciṟi tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(101)
397.	melleḻut tiyiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum.
itu, mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum - ḷakāra īṟu melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy vantu iyiyiṉ ṇakaramāyt tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: muṇñeri, nuṉimaram eṉa varum.
itaṉi vēṟṟumiyiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttuk kōṭaṟkaṇ ciṅkanōkkāka vittaliṉ, alvaḻikkum im muṭipu koḷka. muṇñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum.				(102)
398.		alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēlataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām - ḷakāra īṟu alvaḻik kaṇellām, uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa - tiriyātum ṭakāramāyt tirintum uṟaḻntu muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	muḷkaṭitu muṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.
ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ, kuṇavēṟṟumikkaṇṇum iv vuṟaḻcci koḷka. muḷkuṟumi muṭkuṟumi, ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, kōḷkaṭumi kōṭkaṭumi vāḷkaṭumi vāṭ kaṭumi eṉavum oṭṭuka. 
itaṉāṉē, atōṭkoṇṭāṉ itōṭkoṇṭāṉ utōṭ koṇṭāṉ etōṭ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉavum koḷka.	(103)
399.		āyta niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
					takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, takaram varuvaḻi uṟaḻcciyēyaṉṟi āytamākat tirintum uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: takaram varūuṅ kāliyāṉa - takara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āytam niliyalum varinili yiṉṟu - ḷakāram ṭakāramākat tiritalē yaṉṟi āytamākat tirintu niṟṟalum nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: muḵṭītu, muṭṭītu eṉa varum.	(104)
400.		neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpā kunavum
					vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum
					pōṟṟal vēṇṭu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, mēlataṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpu ākunavum - avvīṟṟu neṭiyataṉ iṟuti tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavaṟṟiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum - vēṟṟumi yallāta viṭattu vēṟṟumiyiṉ iyalpi yuṭiyaṉavāyt tirintu muṭitaliyum, pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumāruḷa - pōṟṟutal vēṇṭuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu..
utāraṇam:	kōḷkaṭitu, vāḷkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum, puṭṭēmpap puyaṉmāṟi (paṭṭiṉappāli. 89) eṉavum varum.
pōṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟataṉāl, utaḷaṅkāy cetiḷ pū tōl eṉa ammup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. utaḷeṉpatu yāṭ ṭiṉi uṇarttuṅkāl muṟkūṟiya muṭipukaḷ iruvaḻikkum ēṟṟa vāṟē muṭikka. utaṭ kōṭu, utaḷkaṭitu, utaṇaṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. ‘nkh¤ijíª jfU KjS k¥gU«’ v‹wh® kuãaÈš (47).(105)
401.		toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu - ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku iruvaḻiyum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟ peyarellām - ḷakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar pōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	tuḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tuḷḷukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
ellā meṉṟataṉāṉē, iruvaḻiyun toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟātu tirintun tiriyātum muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. kōḷkaṭitu kōṭkaṭitu, kōḷkaṭumi kōṭkaṭumi eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa piṟavum varum.
iṉi, vāḷkaṭitu vāṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, vāḷkaṭumi vāṭkaṭumi eṉavuṅ kāṭṭuka. vāḷ-kollutal.	(106)
402.		iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliya ṉiliyum.
itu, tiripu vilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:iruḷ eṉ kiḷavi - iruḷeṉṉuñ col vēṟṟu mip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, veyiliyal niliyum - veyileṉ ṉuñ coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ iruḷiṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
cāriyi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattum oṭṭuka. 	iruḷattu ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ; iruḷiṉ ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(107)
403.		puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
ituvum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, tiripum uṟaḻvum vilakkit toḻiṟpeyarviti vakuttaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷum vaḷḷum - puḷḷeṉṉuñ collum vaḷḷeṉṉuñ collum iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟ peyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum, iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: puḷḷukkaṭitu vaḷḷukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, puḷḷuk kaṭumi vaḷḷuk kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
itaṉit toḻiṟ peyarellām (eḻut. 401) eṉpataṉ piṉ viyātataṉāl, iruvaḻiyum vēṟṟumittiripu eyti muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. puṭkaṭitu vaṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, puṭ kaṭumi vaṭkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, puṇ ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, puṇ ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum varum. puḷḷuvalitu puḷvalitu, puḷḷuvaṉmi puḷvaṉmi eṉa vakarattiṉ muṉṉar ukaram peṟṟum peṟātum varutaliṉ, niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyal pākum (eḻut. 144) eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyāmi uṇarka. itu vaḷḷiṟkum okkum.	(108)
404.		makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
					takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇippeyar (eḻu. 153) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟiya iyalpu vilakkit tiripu vakuttaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccol iṟuti - makka ḷeṉṉum peyarccoliṟuti iyalpēyaṉṟi, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu - takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟākat tirintu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
takkavaḻi yeṉṟār, perumpāṉmi makkaḷ uṭampu uyir nīṅkik kiṭanta kālattiṉ aḵtu immuṭipupeṟum eṉṟaṟku.
utāraṇam: makkaṭki, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
ikkiṭantatu makkaṭṭali eṉpataṉāṉ avvāṟātal koḷka. 51makkaḷki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉat tiriyātu niṉṟatu, uyiruṇmi peṟṟatu. 
iṉic ciṟupāṉmi, makkaṭpaṇpu, makkaṭ cuṭṭu eṉavum varum.			(109)
405.		uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṟ
					kaṇṭuceyaṟ kuriyavi kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṉkaṇ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē ōttākak koṇṭu, cāriyi peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyiyum, eḻuttup peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttuṅ koṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka eṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyal maruṅkiṉ - uṇarakkūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum iyal piṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavi - mēl muṭitta muṭipaṉṟi vaḻak kiṉuṭ kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku uriyavi tōṉṟiyavaḻi, kaṇṇiṉar koḷal - avaṟṟiyuṅ karutik koṇṭu ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	 maṇṇappattam eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu akkup peṟṟatu. maṇṇaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammup peṟṟatu. poṉṉappattam eṉa ṉakara īṟu akkup peṟṟatu. poṉṉaṅ kaṭṭi eṉa ammup peṟṟatu. kāṉaṅkōḻi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi kāṉāṅkōḻi eṉpaṉa marūu. vēyiṉṟali eṉa yakara īṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttuk keṭukka. nīrkuṟitu eṉa rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu. vēr kuṟitu vērkkuṟitu itu rakara īṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci. vaṭacārkkūri mēlcārk kūri ivi valleḻuttu mikka marūu muṭipu. ampark koṇṭāṉ imparkkoṇṭāṉ umparkkoṇṭāṉ empark koṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉa valloṟṟup peṟṟaṉa. takarkkuṭṭi pukarppōttu eṉpaṉa paṇputtoki karutiṟṟēl īṇṭu muṭikka; vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyum. viḻaleṉṉum lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṟakaramākātu ṉakaramāy muṭital koḷka. viḻaṉkāṭu; ceṟu tāḷ puṟam eṉavarum. kallampāṟi, ucilaṅ kōṭu, eliyālaṅkāy, puṭōlaṅkāy eṉa avvīṟu ammup peṟṟatu. kallāmpāṟi eṉpatu marūu. aḻalattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa avvīṟu attup peṟṟatu. aḻukkaṟ pōr, puḻukkaṟcōṟu eṉpaṉa avvīṟṟu alvaḻittiripu. yāḻkuṟitu eṉpatu ḻakara īṟṟu alvaḻi yiyalpu. vīḻkuṟitu vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa avvīṟṟu alvaḻiyuṟaḻcci. tāḻappāvi eṉpatu avvīṟṟu alvaḻi akkup peṟṟatu. yāḻiṉkōṭu ceyki tali puṟam eṉa avvīṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka. muṉṉāḷi vāḻvu, muṉṉāḷip paricu, oru nāḷikkuḻavi, oru tiṅkaḷikkuḻavi eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ḷakāra īṟu aikāramum ataṉōṭu valleḻuttum peṟutal koḷka. piṟavum ivvōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vellām koṇarntu itaṉāṉ muṭikka. kuḷattiṉ puṟam, marattiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutaluṅkoḷka.
iṉik kaṭicol lilli (col. 452) eṉpataṉāṉ, vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum vantu tirintu muṭiyuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa. avaṟṟik kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē eṉpataṉāṉ, maṇṇukkup pōṉāṉ, poṉṉukku viṟṟāṉ, poruḷukkup pōṉāṉ, nellukku viṟṟāṉ, koḷḷukkuk koṇṭāṉ, patiṉēḻu eṉṟāṟpōla vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ukaram peṟuvaṉavum; viṇṇukkumēl, maṇṇukku nāppaṇ 
(tiruk. 162),
pallukkut tōṟṟa paṉimulli piṅkiḷikaḷ
collukkut tōṟṟiṉṉan tōṉṟiṉavā-ṉellukku

eṉṟāṟpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ ukarampeṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum muṭikka.
paṟku neṟku eṉpaṉa mutaliyaṉavuṅ koḷka. ivi urupiṉ poruḷpaṭavārātu urupiṉkaṇ vantaṉavēṉum īṇṭuk kāṭṭiṉām; āṇṭup puḷḷiyiṟutiyum (eḻu. 202) eṉṉum urupiyaṟ cūttirattu ilēcu kōṭaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟeṉṟu karuti. iṉi, accūttirattut tēruṅ kāli eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittalum oṉṟu.			(110)
puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	ukaramoṭu puṇarum eṉpatu. tokimarapu 21-m cūttirattu yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi ukaram peṟāveṉṟu vitittati.
2.	īṇṭu vēṟṟumi oḻittu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟateṉṟatu. nakaraviṟuti valliṉam varuṅkāl vēṟṟumiyiṉum alvaḻiyiṉum ukaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikkum. ña na ma va vantāl ukarampeṟṟum muṭiyumeṉṟu māṭṭēṟṟāṟ koḷḷa niṟṟaliṉ. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷātu alvaḻikku māttiram māṭṭēṟṟik koḷka eṉṟapaṭi. nakaraviṟutikku vēṟṟumi muṭipu varuñ cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa.
3.	porun eṉpatu cātippeyar eṉṟatu. porunar eṉṉuñ cātiyi yuṇartti niṉṟateṉṟapaṭi. viṉippeyar - toḻiṟpeyar.
4.	nakara ikaramē iṭṭeḻutupa eṉṟatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ? veriṉ eṉa ṉakara vikaramiṭṭu eḻutuvāri vilakkutaṟku.
5.	iraṇṭāvataṉṟiripu eṉṟatu. tokimarapu 15-m cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tiripi.
6.	irutiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭital eṉṟatu. āṇvantāṉ āṇ vantatu eṉa muṭiyāmal irutiṇikkaṇṇum āṇvantatu eṉa muṭitali.
7.	viravuppeyareṉṟatu - āṇ eṉṉum potuppeyari.
8.	atikāra valleḻuttiṉmi eṉṟatu - uyirīṟṟukkāyiṉ atikāra valleḻuttu uṇṭu; itu meyyīṟāṉapaṭiyāl atikāra valleḻut tiṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi. ātaliṉ valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka eṉṟapaṭi. uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉṉaṉṟi ya ra ḻa voḻinta meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ valliṉam mikāteṉka.
9.	maṇṇutal - kaḻuvutal. veṇ - ōrāṟu. eṇṇuppāṟu - eṇṇiṉ kaṭpāṟu eṉaviriyum. eṇ - eḷ. pāṟu - paruntu.
10.	maṇṇappattam - maṇṇālākiyapattam. pantam - pattam eṉṟāyiṟṟu. pantam - tiraḷ. eṇṇālākiya noli. nōli - appavaki. maṇṇappattam, eṇṇanoli ivi akkuppeṟṟaṉa.
11.	`moḻimutalākum' eṉṟatu. tokimarapu 5-m cūttiratti.
12.	tokimarapiṟ kūṟiya..... iyalpiṉ kaṇṇumeṉṟatu. toki marapu 5-m cūttirattiṉkaṭ kūṟiya iyalpu muṭipi. īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṭ ṭirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉ eṉṟatu. ippuḷḷi mayaṅkiyal 6-m cūttirattuk kūṟiya tiripu muṭipiyum tokimarapu 6=m cūttirattuk kūṟiya iyalpu muṭipiyum.
13.	num - viravuppeyar.
14.	mēl eṉṟatu - varuñcūttiratti. ilēcu eṉṟatu - vaḻakkattāṉ eṉpati.
15.	oṟṟeṉṟatu - nilimoḻi makaratti.
16.	āṇikūṟalāvatu - ivvāciriyaṉatu karuttu ituveṉak koḷḷa vittal. pakarattiṟku iṉam makaramātaliṟ ṟiriyātu eṉpatu karuttu.
17.	ivi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu muṭintaṉa eṉṟatu - ulakki eṉpatil ukaramum mutala eṉpatil marakamum iyalpukaṇamātal paṟṟi. meṉkaṇam, iṭikkaṇam, uyirkkaṇam eṉṉum ivikaḷ iyalpu kaṇaṅkaḷ. iyalpukaṇameṉṟatu - īṇṭu ukara makaraṅkaḷi. kolluṅ koṟṟaṉ, uṇṇuñcāttaṉ makaran tirintaṉa. kavaḷamāntu malikeḻu nāṭaṉ mutaliyaṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟaṉa. kollum yāṉi mutaliyaṉa makaram nilipeṟṟaṉa.
18.	`alvaḻiyellām' eṉṟatu - itaṟku mutaṟcūttiratti.
19.	atikārattāṉum eṉṟatu - alvaḻi atikārappaṭṭu niṉṟamiyi. poruḷ eṉṟatu - uḷḷaṅkiyeṉap poruḷ tarutali.
20.	viṉikkuṟippup peyareṉṟatu. kuṟippākak kālaṅkāṭṭum mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyari. paṭu eṉpatu uṇṭākiṉṟa eṉak kuṟippākak kālaṅkāṭṭaṭal paṟṟik kuṟippueṉṟār. ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉṟu poruḷurikkuṅkāl paṟṟutaṟku mutaṉili yiṉṟeṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. paṭu eṉpati viṉit tokiyākak koḷḷiṉ iraṇṭu kālattukkum potuvākum. iraṇṭu kālam eṉṟatu iṟappu nikaḻviṉi. paṭu eṉpatu iraṇṭukālamum paṟṟi viriyuṅkāl paṭṭapaṭum eṉa viriyum. avviraṇṭun bjkkamujṉilipaLe‹patu.paL eṉpati mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyarāka vittu varumoḻiyāka āciriyar eṭuttōtiṉāraṉṟi viṉit tokiyāyiṉ paṭupulavar eṉpatil paṭu eṉpatip pirittu varumoḻiyākak kūṟāreṉpatu karutimutaṉiliyāyniṟṟaliṉataṉieṭuttōtiṉāreṉṟār.paL eṉpatu paṭṭa eṉpataṟku mutaṉiliyākumēyaṉṟi paṟṟa eṉpataṟku mutaṉiliyākāteṉpār. iraṇṭu mutaṉili kūṭi oṉṟāyniṉṟu gṟṟutalicbcayyappaṭṭavṉapbpāruḷjrāmiyāṉumvṉṟār.ï§nf paṟṟutaṟkuriya mutaṉili yilli. ātaliṉ paṭu eṉpatu paṭṭa eṉap poruḷ tarumaṉṟip paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉap poruḷtarāmiyiṉ cirayāciriyarkaUttupgruntājṉpatukaUttu.
21.	avviraṇṭu ilēcu eṉṟatu - vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ eṉṟatiyum āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟatiyum.
22.	tamakāṇam - āṟām vēṟṟumi akara urupu.
23.	num toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum moḻiyaṉmiyiṉ ilēciṉāl muṭittār.
24.	kuṟiyīr, kuṟiyam eṉpavaṟṟi,  ellāṅ kuṟiyīr ellāṅ kuṟiyam eṉa oṭṭuka. kuṟiyīr eṉpatu muṉṉiliyik kāṭṭavum, kuṟiyayam eṉpatu taṉmiyik kāṭṭavum vanta varumoḻikaḷ.  
25.	cem, tum eṉpaṉa mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar.
26.	nāṭṭam, āṭṭam eṉpaṉa vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
27.	muṟkūṟiya valleḻutteṉṟatu. 32-m cūttirattāṟ peṟṟa valleḻutti.
28.	kuṇavēṟṟumi eṉṟatu - kaṭumi mutaliya kuṇac coṟkaḷ vantu puṇarum vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyi.
29.	āṉ -avviṭam. īṉ - ivviṭam. ūṉ - uvviṭam.
30.	ap peyari eṉṟatu - piṉ muṉ eṉpavaṟṟi.
31.	kuyiṉ viṉi eṉṟatu - kuyiṉutali yuṇarttum mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyari. kuyiṉutal - tuḷiceytal; aḻuttutal.
32.	paṇpu - paṇputtoki. paṇputtokiyāṅkāl ekiṉākiya cēvaleṉa viriyum.

34.	mēl eṉṟatu 46-m cūttiratti. atiluḷḷa `māṉamilli' eṉṟataṉāṉ ................. eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avi iccūttirattu `iṟutiyōṭuṟaḻum' eṉṟa uṟaḻcci iyalpil aṭaṅkumeṉṟapaṭi.
35.	miṉ eṉpatu. miṉṉutaṟ ṟoḻilaṉṟi miṉṉali uṇarttu miṭattup peyarām. atupaṟṟip poruḷ eṉṟār. piṉ - piṉṉutaliyum, piṉṉiya kūntaliyu muṇarttum. paṉ - collutaliyum colliyu muṇarttum. kaṉ - kaṉṉāṉtoḻiliyum kaṉṉāṉiyumuṇarttum.
36.	kuṇavēṟṟumi eṉṟatu - kaṉṉakkaṭumi eṉpatiṟ pōla vēṟṟumik kaṭ paṇpuccol vantu puṇartali. kaṭumi - paṇpu.
37.	koṟṟaṅkuṭi - itu aṉkeṭṭu ammuppeṟṟut tirintu muṭintatu. vēṭṭamaṅkalam - `vēṭaṉ' aṉkeṭṭu ammuppeṟṟu makaraṅkeṭṭu ṭakaramiraṭṭi vēṭṭamaṅkalameṉṟāyiṟṟu.
38.	taṟpukaḻ mutaliyaṉa - āṟām vēṟṟumi.

40.	pīr, koṭi yeṉpatu ivar karuttuppōlum. marameṉpārumuḷar eṉṟamiyiṉ. kutir mutaliyaṉa maram.
41.	itaṉi eṉṟatu - kāḻi.
42.	ataṉāl eṉpatu - attāl eṉa niṉṟatupōlum. ēṉiyavumaṉṉa.

50.	tāmari - ai iṟuti; veḷḷam - am iṟuti. ām pal - pal iṟuti.

kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟu niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyal piṉi yuṇarttuvatu ivviyalākum. neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaram varum eṉa moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟukaratti ayaliluḷḷa eḻuttu vakiyāl uyirttoṭar, iṭittoṭar, āytat toṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar eṉa aintākappakuttu, avaṟṟoṭu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉvaruṅ kuṟṟukarattiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakippaṭuttu viḷakkukiṉṟār.
yaraḻa muṉṉark kacatapaṅañanama īroṟṟāki varuṅ kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ oruvāṟṟāṉ iṭiyoṟṟukkaḷōṭu toṭarntuvariṉum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyaṭuttu nērē toṭarntaṉa allavātaliṉ avi iṭittoṭarākak koḷḷappaṭāveṉṟum, āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum iruvaḻiyilum keṭātu niṟintē niṟku meṉṟum, vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu muṉkūṟiya (arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukum) iyalpil niṟṟalumuritteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiṉmuṉ yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkeṭa aṅku ōr ikaram vantu kuṟuki niṟkumeṉṟum ivviyaliṉ mutal aintu cūttirattāṉum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyalpiṉi āciriyar potuvakiyāṟ kūṟippōntār. 6-mutal 17-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟiṉār. 18-mutal kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcciyiṉit toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār.
uṇmit taṉmiyi yuṇarttum uṇṭu eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ iṟutiniṉṟa ukaram mey yoṭuṅkeṭa ṇakaram ḷakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum, kuṟṟukara vīṟṟut ticippeyarkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu perunticiyum tammiṟ puṇarumiṭattu ēyeṉṉuñcāriyi iṭiyē vantumuṭiyu meṉṟum, kuṟṟukaravīṟṟup perunticikaḷōṭu kōṇat ticikaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iṟutiyukaram meyyoṭuṅ koṭu meṉṟum avvaḻi teṟku eṉpataṉ ṟakaram ṉakaramākattiriyu meṉṟu«kūṟuva®āciriyar.
kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyariyalpiṉi 28-ām cūttira mutalākattoṭaṅki¡kūṟukiṉṟār. pattu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ muṉ oṉṟumutal eṭṭuvariyuḷḷa eṇkaḷum āyirameṉṉum eṇṇum niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum vantu puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 28-mutal 30-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ uṇarttuvaṉa oṉṟu mutal eṭṭuvariyuḷḷa kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇum peyarkaḷiṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇartali 31-mutal 38-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvaṉa. oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇaruṅkāl nilimoḻiyākiya oṉpatil okarattiṟku mēlākat takara meytōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patu keṭṭu ṉakaram ṇakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭittut toṇṇ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉpatil pakaravuyirmeyyum āytamumkeṭṭu ūkāram tōṉṟi iṟutiyiluḷḷa tu eṉpataṉ takaram ṟakaramākat tirintu ūṟu eṉṟākit toṇṇuṟu eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum, ivviyal 39-ām cūttirattāl āciriyar viritturippar. mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum vantu puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 40-mutal 53-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum, nūṟeṉṉuñcol varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarvataṉi 54, 55, 56, 57-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum, āyiram eṉṉum eṇ taṉittum nūṟeṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttum puṇartali 58-mutal 65-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum kūṟuvar āciriyar. oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ nūṟu eṉṉum eṇ vantupuṇariṉ muṟkūṟiyavāṟu nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟa oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ okarattiṉ muṉṉē takaramey oṉṟu tōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patukeṭṭu ṉakaramey ḷakara meyyāka iraṭṭittut toḷḷ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉpataṉ mutalil niṉṟa nakaramey keṭa ammeyyiṉ mēlēṟiya ūkāram ākāramākattirintu ataṉiyaṭuttu ikaravuyirum rakaravuyir meyyum iṭiyētōṉṟi īṟṟiluḷṟaṟakara vukaraṅkeṭṭu makarameytōṉṟi āyiram eṉṟākit toḷḷāyiram eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum 57-ām cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. toṇṇuṟu, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷukku āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟum puṇarcci muṟikaḷ valintu kūṟuvaṉavākavē amintuḷḷaṉa veṉṟum oṉpatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭat tolkāppiyarē toṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarit tam nūlil āṇṭiruttalāl toṇṭu+pattu = toṇṇuṟu eṉṟum, toṇṭu+āyiram = toḷḷāyiram eṉṟum nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu puṇarcciviti kūṟuvatē moḻiyiyalpukku ottatākumeṉṟum paritimāṟkaliñar kūṟum koḷki iṅku cintikkat takkatākum.
nūṟu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka ataṉ muṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpatum aviyūr pattum aḷavum niṟiyum vantu puṇarum muṟiyiṉi 66-mutal 68-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ viḷakku vaṉavām. ōṉṟu mutal eṭṭiṉiyūrnta paḵteṉṉum eṇṇup peyaruṭaṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpateṇkaḷum āyiramum aḷavum niṟiyum puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 69, 70, 71-ām cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭ peyarkaḷ puṇarumiyalpiṉi 72, 73-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa eṇkaḷiṉmuṉ aḷavu, niṟi, eṇ eṉpavaṟṟukkuriya mā eṉṉuñcol puṇartali 74-ām cūttirattālum āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. eṇṇuppeyar kaḷuḷ mikka eṇṇōṭu kuṟinta eṇ vantu puṇartali ummit tokiyākavum, kuṟinta eṇṇōṭu mikka eṇ varutalip paṇput tokiyākavumkoṇṭu āciriyar viti kūṟiyuḷḷami ivaṇ karutaṟkuriyatākum. lakāra ṉakāra vīṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟuvatu 75-ām cūttiram. iv vatikārattu nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ iviyeṉat tokutturippatu 76-ām cūttiramākum. ivviyaliṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa 77-ām cūttiram eḻuttatikārattiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 161-166

oṉpatāvatu
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
406.		īreḻut torumoḻi yuyirttoṭa riṭittoṭa
					rāytat toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭa
					rāyiru mūṉṟē yukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ.
eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttuk kuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa eḻuttup poruṭpeyarōṭum eṇṇuppeyar mutaliyavaṟ ṟōṭum puṇarum muṟimi uṇarttiṉamiyiṟ kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu. itu meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala (eḻu. 103) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, uyiriṉatu vikāramāy niṉṟa kuṟṟu karatti iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇarkkiṉṟamiyiṉ, mēli ōttiṉōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu. ittalic cūttiram - moḻimarapiṉakattu iruvaḻiya eṉṟa kuṟṟukaram, itaṉakattu iṉittu moḻiyiṟuti varumeṉṟu avaṟṟiṟkup peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. appeyar peyar, ammuṟi muṟi, attoki toki. toṭar moḻi yīṟṟu (eḻu. 36) varumeṉṟu āṇṭuk kūṟiyavataṉi īṇṭu aintu vakippaṭutti, ataṉoṭu neṭṭeḻut timparum (eḻu. 36) eṉṟatu oṉṟē yātaliṉ ataṉiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakit teṉṟār.
itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttorumoḻi - iraṇ ṭeḻuttāṉākiya oru moḻiyum, uyirttoṭar - uyirmēlvarum meyyit  toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, iṭittoṭar - iṭiyoṟṟu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āytat toṭarmoḻi-āytamākiya eḻuttu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, vaṉṟoṭar-valloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, meṉṟoṭar - melloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyiru mūṉṟē-ākiya avvāṟu collumē, ukaraṅ kuṟuku iṭaṉ - ukaraṅ kuṟukivarum iṭam eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum.
1itaṉi ēḻeṉṟu koḷvārkkup piṇṇākku, cuṇṇāmpu, āmaṇakku mutaliyaṉa muṭiyāmi uṇarka.	(1)
407.		avaṟṟuḷ
					īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi yiṭittoṭa rākā.
iḵtu - avvāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-avvāṟaṉuḷ, īroṟṟut toṭar moḻi-iraṇṭoṟṟu iṭikkaṇ toṭarntu niṟkuñ colliṟku iṭiyiṉa oṟṟu mutal niṉṟāl, iṭit toṭarākā - mēl iṭiyiṉan toṭarntu nillā, valliṉamum melliṉamun toṭarntu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ārkku, īrkku, noympu, moympu eṉa varum.			(2)
408.		allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu
					mellā viṟutiyu mukara niliyum.2
iḵtu - iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē (eḻu. 37) eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟuku meṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki  avviyal niliyu mēṉi mūṉṟē (eḻu. 12) eṉṟa vitiyē peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattum, vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ellā iṟutiyum ukaram 3niliyum - āṟu īṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum ukaran taṉ ari māttiriyip peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
varumoḻiyāṉallatu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyum viḷaṅ kāmiyiṉ, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum eṉavē irumoḻip puṇarcci eṉpatu peṟṟām. ivviru moḻikkaṇ paḻiya arimāttiri peṟṟē niṟkumeṉṟār. aṉṟi irumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ oru māttiri peṟumeṉ ṟārkkup paṉmoḻip puṇarcciyākiya ceyyuḷilak kaṇattu kuṟṟukarattāṉ nērpaci niripaci kōṭalum, avaṟṟāṉ aṟupatu vañciccīr kōṭalum, pattoṉpatiṉā yirat tirunūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟoru toṭi kōṭalum ilavāy muṟṟiyalukaramākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭutaliṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.
utāraṇam: nākukaṭitu, varakukaṭitu, nākukaṭumi, varakukaṭumi eṉa varum. ivi, tam arimāttiri peṟṟaṉa. ēṉiya vaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
iṉi, itu mālyāṟupōntu kālcurantupāyntu eṉat toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇum arimāttiri peṟṟatu eṉṉākkāl vañciccīriṉṟāmāṟu uṇarka.	(3)
409.valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻit
				tolli yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.
itu, muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ arimāttiri peṟum eṉṟataṉi vilakki iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉum (eḻu. 37) eṉṟataṉāṉ ari māttiriyiṉum kuṟukumeṉṟu āṇṭu vitittatu īṇṭu valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇē varumeṉ kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi - val loṟṟut toṭar moḻikkuṟṟukaram, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi-valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, tolli iyaṟki niliyalum urittu- iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukum (eḻu. 37) eṉpata ṉāṟ kūṟiya arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟa iyalpilē niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
ummi  etirmaṟi.
utāraṇam:	kokkukkaṭitu kokkukkaṭumi eṉa ari māttiri yiṟ kuṟintavāṟu, kuraṅkukaṭi teṉpatu mutaliya vaṟṟoṭu paṭuttuc cevikaruviyāka uṇarka.
muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattu 4ukara niṟiyu meṉṟu pāṭam ōti, ataṟku ukaram arimāttiriyiṟ 5ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, iccūttirattiṟkup paḻiya arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.	(4)
410.		yakaram varuvaḻi yikaram kuṟuku
					mukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu.
itu, kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattu varumāṟu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yakaram varuvaḻi ukarakkiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu - yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu nilimoḻik kuṟṟukara veḻuttu 6muṟṟat tōṉṟātu, ikaraṅ kuṟukum - āṇṭu ōr ikaram vantu arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

411.		īreḻuttu moḻiyu muyirttoṭar moḻiyum
					vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉoṟṟiṭi yiṉamikat
					tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.
itu, muṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷ muṉṉar niṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum -  īreḻut torumoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, iṉoṟṟu iṭimika - iṉamākiya oṟṟu iṭiyilē mika, valleḻuttu mikuti tōṟṟam vēṇṭum - valleḻuttu mikuti tōṉṟi muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ   eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: yāṭṭukkāl, cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, muyiṟṟuk kāl ciṉi tali puṟam eṉavum varum. kayiṟṟuppuṟam, vayiṟṟuttī eṉpaṉavumām.
tōṟṟam eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēṉikkaṇattum immuṭipu koḷka. e-ṭu:	yāṭṭuñāṟci niṇam maṇi vāl ataḷ eṉavum, muyiṟṟu ñāṟci niṇam muṭṭi valimi aṭi āṭṭam eṉavum varum.			(6)
412.		oṟṟiṭi yiṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
					yattiṟat tilli valleḻuttu mikalē.

attiṟa meṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉikkaṇattum oṟṟiṭi mikā mi koḷka. nākuñāṟci; nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉa oṭṭuka. (7)
413.		iṭiyoṟṟut toṭaru māytat toṭaru

417.		īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭaru
					mammiṭi varaṟku muriyavi yuḷavē
					yammara poḻuku moḻivayi ṉāṉa.

ammarapoḻukum eṉṟataṉāl, 10aracakkaṉṉi, muracak kaṭippu eṉa akkum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum; araca vāḻkki, muraca vāḻkki eṉa akkuk koṭuttum muṭikka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē iruṭṭattuk koṇṭāṉ, viḷakkattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum; mayilāppiṟ koṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉa iṉ koṭuttum; kariyataṉkōṭu, neṭiyataṉ kōṭu eṉa aṉ koṭuttum muṭikka. 418.		oṟṟunili tiriyā takkoṭu varūu
					makkiḷi moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu, meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟu nili tiriyātu akkoṭum varūum - oṟṟu muṉṉiṉṟa nilitiriyātu akkuccāriyi yōṭum piṟa cāriyiyōṭum varum, akkiḷi moḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - akkiḷiyāṉa coṟkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṟku ummiyi eṉa muṉṉar māṟuka.
utāraṇam: kuṉṟakkūki, maṉṟappeṇṇi eṉavarum. ummiyāṟ  koṅkattuḻavu, vaṅkattuvāṇikam eṉa attum peṟṟaṉa.
nili yeṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟu nilitiriyā atikārattuk kaṇ varuñ cāriyikku iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. akkiḷi yeṉṟār, iraṇṭu cāriyi toṭarntu muṭivaṉa vum uḷaveṉṟaṟku. pārppaṉakkuḻavi, cēri, tōṭṭam, piḷḷi eṉa aṉṉum akkum vantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku uṭimi virikka. pārppiṉuṭkuḻavi eṉṟu mām. pārppāṉākiyakuḻavi eṉṟāl 11īṇṭu muṭiyāteṉṟu uṇarka. pārppaṉa makaṉ, pārppaṉa vaṉiti eṉpaṉavum 12pārp pāṉ cāti uṇarttiṉa. 419.		eṇṇuppeyark kiḷavi yurupiya ṉiliyum.
itu, kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu poruṭpeyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.

420.		vaṇṭum peṇṭu miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

421.		peṇṭeṉ kiḷavik kaṉṉum variyār.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: peṇṭaṉki, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.		(16)
422.		yāte ṉiṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
					vāyta viṟutiyu murupiya ṉiliyum.
iḵtu - īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ oṉ ṟaṟkum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarat tiṟkum vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yātu eṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟuti yum - yāteṉṉum īṟuñ cuṭṭeḻuttu mutalākiya āytattoṭar moḻikkuṟṟiyalukara īṟum ,urupiyal niliyum –urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇa niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟuc cuṭṭumutaliṟuti āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: yātaṉkōṭu, ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉ kōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum. āytaṅkeṭā muṉṉē aṉṉiṉ akarattik kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl ēṟṟuka; āytaṅkeṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ.	(17)
423.		muṉṉuyir varumiṭat tāytap puḷḷi
					maṉṉal vēṇṭu malvaḻi yāṉa.
itu, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutikku oruvaḻi alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: muṉṉuyir varumiṭattu - cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, āytap puḷḷi maṉṉal vēṇṭum - āytavoṟṟu muṉpōlak keṭātu nili peṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ, alvaḻiyāṉa - alvaḻikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa niṟutti; aṭi āṭi ili īyam ural ūrti eḻu ēṇi aiyam oṭukkam ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
muṉṉeṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka. 14aḵtaṭivu, aḵtoṭṭam eṉa oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭā murupu virikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ēṉi ilakkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka.	(18)
424.		ēṉimuṉ variṉē tāṉili yiṉṟē.
itu, mēlavaṟṟiṟkup piṟakaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi muṉvariṉ - muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar uyirkkaṇa mallaṉa varumāyiṉ, tāṉ niliyiṉṟu - avvāytaṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.

425.		allatu kiḷappi ṉellā moḻiyuñ
					colliya paṇpi ṉiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu āṟu īṟṟukkuṟṟukarattiṟkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉ - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattu, ellā moḻiyum - āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum, colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkiyākum - mēl āciriyaṉ kūṟiya kuṇattiyuṭiya iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nākukaṭitu, varakukaṭitu, teḷkukaṭitu, eḵkukaṭitu, kuraṅku kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. ēṉik kaṇattuk kaṇ niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyal pākum           (eḻut. 144) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
ellāmoḻiyum eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉiccollum viṉik kuṟippuc collum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. kiṭantatu kutiri, karitu kutiri eṉa varum.
colliya eṉṟataṉāṉ, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput toki vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭitalum koḷka. karaṭṭukkāṇam, kuruṭṭukkōḻi, tiruṭṭup puliyaṉ, kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟi, veḷiṟṟuppaṉi, eyiṟṟuppal eṉavum; varaṭṭāṭu, kuruṭṭerutu eṉavum varum.
paṇpiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākātu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka..  	 ōr yāṭṭi yāṉi, aiyāṭṭi yerutu eṉavum; aṟṟik kūttar, iṟṟikkūttar eṉavum; maṉṟit tūti maṉṟip pāṉi, paṇṭic cāṉṟār eṉavum varum.	(20)
426.	valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu avvāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikum - valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟu valleḻuttu varuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	kokkukkaṭitu, pākkukkaṭitu, paṭṭukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(21)
427.		cuṭṭuccuṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
					yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyu
					māyiya ṟiriyā valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu - av āṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭuc ciṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - cuṭṭākiya ciṉiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟum, yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - yāveṉṉum viṉā mutalākiya meṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, valleḻutti yaṟki ā iyal tiriyā - valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyun taṉmi yākiya avviyalpiṉ tiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iyaṟki yeṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam iyalpāyum, vaṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam mikkum muṭivaṉa koḷka. iruntukoṇṭāṉ, āṇṭuceṉṟāṉ, tantu tīrntāṉ, vantupōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; cettuk kiṭantāṉ, ceṟṟuc ceytāṉ, uyttuk koṇṭāṉ, naṭṭup pōṉāṉ eṉavum varum.			(22)
428.		yāviṉā moḻiyē yiyalpu mākum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttup peṟutalē aṉṟi iyalpā meṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum  - avaṟṟuḷ yāveṉṉum viṉāviyuṭiya col muṟkūṟiya vāṟaṉṟi iyal pāyum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

429.		annāṉ moḻiyun tannili tiriyā.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: annāṉmoḻiyum - cuṭṭumutaṉ mūṉṟum yāmutaṉ moḻiyumākiya annāṉku moḻiyum, tam nili tiriyā - tam melloṟṟāya taṉmi tirintu valloṟṟākātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	muṟkāṭṭiyavē. tannili yeṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟut tiriyātu valle ḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka. aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, uṅkuk koṇ ṭāṉ, eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

430.		uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi yuṇmi ceppiṉ

431.		irutici puṇari ṉēyiṭi varumē.

432.		tiripuvēṟu kiḷappi ṉoṟṟu mukaramuṅ

					teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅ kāli yāṉa.

433.		oṉṟumuta lāka veṭṭa ṉiṟuti
					yellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṟ
					kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
					muṟṟa viṉvarūu miraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē.
itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum poruṭ peyarkkaṇ puṇarumāṟu uṇartti, iṉi avvīṟṟu eṇṇup peyar muṭikkiṉṟār. iḵtu avaṟṟuṭ patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarōṭu eṇṇuppeyar vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭaṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇ mutalāka eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇīṟāyuḷḷa ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, pattaṉmuṉ variṉ - patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉvariṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum - appat teṉṉuñ colliṟ kuṟṟiyalukaran tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭum, iraṇṭalaṅkaṭi muṟṟa iṉvarūum - āṇṭu iraṇṭallāta eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṭattu muṭiya iṉ cāriyi iṭivantu puṇarumeṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: patiṉoṉṟu, patiṉmūṉṟu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉintu, patiṉāṟu, patiṉēḻu, patiṉeṭṭu eṉa varum.
nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṟ, piṟaveṇṇiṉ muṉṉarp piṟa peyar vantuḻiyum iṉpeṟutal koḷka. oṉpatiṉkūṟu, oṉpatiṉpāl eṉa varum.  muṟṟa veṉpataṉāṟ, patiṉāṉ keṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram varumoḻikkaṭ karuviceytu keṭuttu muṭikka.	(28)
434.		patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa ṉakāra miraṭṭa
					lotta teṉpa viraṇṭuvaru kāli.
itu, mēl iṉ peṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya iraṇṭaṟkup piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: pattaṉ oṟṟuk keṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal - patteṉ ṉuñ colliṉ niṉṟa takara oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittu varutal, iraṇṭu varukāli ottateṉpa - iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇ varuṅkālattiṟ poruntiṟ ṟeṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum.
kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭuṅ keṭum (eḻu. 433) eṉṟa viti itaṟkum mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka.	(29)
435.		āyiram variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
iḵtu āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar variṉ varum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya pattaṉ muṉṉar oṉṟu mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟi āyira meṉṉu meṇ vantālum īṟukeṭṭu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpil tiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: patiṉāyiram eṉa varum.

436.		niṟiyu maḷavum varūuṅ kāliyuṅ
					kuṟiyā tāku miṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu niṟippeyarum aḷavup peyarum puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: niṟiyum aḷavum varūuṅ kāliyum - muṟkūṟiya patteṉ pataṉ muṉṉar niṟippeyarum aḷavup peyarum varuṅ kālattum, iṉṉeṉ cāriyi kuṟiyātākum - avviṉṉeṉ ṉuñ cāriyi kuṟiyātu vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: patiṉkaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum; patiṉkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi eṉavum; patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉavum varum.
kuṟiyātāku meṉpataṉāṟ, poruṭpeyarum eṇṇup peyarum niṟippeyarum vantuḻiyum iṉ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceytu muṭikka. 	patiṟṟu vēli, yāṇṭu, aṭukku, muḻam eṉavum, patiṉ tiṅkaḷ eṉavum, patiṟṟuttoṭi eṉavum varum. patiṟ ṟoṉṟu eṉpatupōla iraṇṭumutaṟ pattaḷavum oṭṭuka.
ivvīṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal 15aḷavākumoḻimutal (eḻu. 121) eṉpataṉuḷ niliiya eṉṟataṉāl muṭikka, ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum ukaram varutalum valleḻuttup peṟutalum oṉṟu mutalāka (eḻu. 433) eṉpataṉuḷ muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.	(31)
437.		oṉṟumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
					niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam
					vantiṭi niliyu miyaṟkit teṉpa
					kūṟiya viyaṟki kuṟṟiya lukara
					māṟa ṉiṟuti yalvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu īṟākak kūṟukiṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, niṉṟa pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa - varumoḻiyāka vantuniṉṟa patteṉṉuñ colliṉatu takara oṟṟukkeṭa, āytam vantu ilniliyum iyaṟkitteṉg –āytamāṉatu vantu iṭiya nilipeṟum iyalyiyuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻiyāṉa –avaṟṟuḷ āṟeṉṉum īṟallāta viṭattu, kuṟṟiyalukaram kūṟiyiyaṟk –kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟkūṟiya iyaṟkiyāymeyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
iṅṅaṉam varumāṟu mēṟcūttiraṅkaḷuṭ kāṭṭutum.
vanteṉṟataṉāl, āytamākat tiriyātu takara oṟṟuk keṭṭu orupatu eṉṟu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.	(32)

					tōṉṟiya vakarat tiyaṟki yākum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum - mūṉṟeṉ ṉum eṇṇum, nāṉkeṉṉu meṇṇum ainteṉṉu meṇṇum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkiyākum - muṉṉart tōṉṟi niṉṟa vakaram varumoḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpāka mūṉṟiṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum, nāṉkiṉkaṇ lakara oṟṟāyum, aintiṉkaṇ oṟṟuk keṭṭum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	muvvakal, muvvuḻakku eṉa varum. itaṟkut tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl, oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. nālakal, nāluḻakku, aiyakal, aiyuḻakku eṉa varum.
tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl mūṉṟeṉpatu mutal nīṇṭa iṭattu nilimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭuttukkoḷka. iyaṟki eṉṟataṉāṉ, toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē (eḻut. 452) mutaliya mūṉṟu cūttiramuṅ koṇarntu muṭikka.	(51)
457.	mūṉṟaṉ KtaṉiliÚṭaluKrittē					yuḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkat tāṉa.
itu, muṉṉark kuṟukumeṉṟataṉi nīṇṭu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalum urittu - mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa eḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu, aḵti yāṇ ṭeṉiṉ, uḻakku eṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa - uḻakkeṉṉuñ coṉ muṭiyum vaḻakkiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: mūvuḻakku eṉa varum.
vaḻakkattāṉa eṉpataṉāṉ, akaleṉ kiḷavikku mutaṉili nīṭaluṅ koḷka. mūvakal eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, nili moḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭukka.
mūḻakku mūḻākkeṉṉum marūmuṭivu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka.	(52)
458.		āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutaṉī ṭummē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āṟeṉ kiḷavi - āṟeṉṉu meṇṇup peyar akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa variṉ, mutal nīṭum - muṉṉark kuṟuki niṉṟa mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
aṟuveṉṉātu āṟeṉṟār, tirintataṉṟiripatu eṉṉum nayattāl.
utāraṇam: āṟakal, āṟuḻakku eṉa varum.	(53)
459.		oṉpā ṉiṟuti yurupunili tiriyā
					tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi moḻiyē.
itu, kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭātu niṉṟu iṉ peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti uruvunili tiriyātu - aḷavum niṟiyum varuvaḻi oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaran taṉ vaṭivu nili tiriyātu niṉṟu, cāriyi moḻi iṉpeṟal vēṇṭum - cāriyic collākiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	oṉpatiṉkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
cāriyi moḻi yeṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉṉōṭu ukaramum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttuc ceyki ceytu muṭikka. oṉpatiṟṟuk kalam, cāṭi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
uruvu peṉpataṉāṉ, oṉpatiṟṟeṉa 19oṟṟiraṭṭutal ellāvaṟ ṟiṟkuṅ koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, oṉpatiṉāḻi yeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakarak kēṭuṅ koḷka. aḷavāku moḻi mutal (eḻu. 121) eṉpataṉkaṇ, niliiyeṉṉum ilēcāṉum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.	(54)
460.		nūṟumuṉ variṉuṅ kūṟiya viyalpē.
iḵtu - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉpataṉip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, nūṟuvariṉum - nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar vantālum, kūṟiya iyalpu - mēṟ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
atu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramāy ukaram peṟṟu, iṭinili rakaram iraṇṭaṉkaṭ keṭṭu muṭitalām.
utāraṇam: orunūṟu, irunūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. ivi māṭṭēṟṟāṉ muṭintaṉa. 20māṭṭēṟu ovvātaṉa mēṟkūṟi muṭippa.	(55)
461.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.
itu, māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟumuṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāramākum - mūṉṟā meṇ ṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu nakaravoṟṟākum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: munnūṟu eṉa varum.	(56)
462.		nāṉku mintu moṟṟumey tiriyā.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā-nāṉkeṉṉu meṇṇum ainteṉṉu meṇṇum tammoṟṟukkaḷ nili tiri yātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu eṉa varum.
mey yeṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṉūṟu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi nakarattuḷ ūkāram pirittu, laṉaveṉa varūum (eḻu. 149) eṉpataṉāṉ ṉakara voṟṟākki, ūkāramēṟṟi, nilimoḻi ṉakaraṅkeṭuttuk koḷka.	(57)
463.		oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntukiḷan taṟṟē
					munti yoṟṟē ḷakāra miraṭṭu
					nūṟeṉ kiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa
					ūvā vāku miyaṟkit teṉpa
					vāyiṭi varuta likāra rakāra
					mīṟumey keṭuttu makara moṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntu kiḷantaṟṟu - oṉpa teṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa okaram mēṟ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiyavāṟu pōla oru takaram oṟṟi ataṉmēl ēṟimuṭiyum, munti oṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum - avvokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ḷakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, nūṟeṉkiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa ū āvākum iyaṟkit teṉpa - varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇup peyar nakāramākiya meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiya ūkāram ākāramām iyalpi yuṭitteṉpar pulavar, āyiṭi ikāra rakāram varutal - am moḻiyiṭi ōr ikaramum rakaramum varuka, īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum - īṟākiya kuṟṟukarattiṉiyum aḵtu ēṟiniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉiyum keṭuttu oru makara oṟṟu vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
mey yeṉpataṉāṉ, nilimoḻikkaṭ pakaraṅ keṭukka.
utāraṇam: toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum.
itaṉi oṉpateṉṉum okarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta takara oṟṟiṉ mēlē okarattiyēṟṟip, pakaraṅ keṭuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟiṉi iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟākki, nūṟeṉpataṉ nakaraṅkeṭuttu, ūkāram ākāramākki ḷakarattiṉ mēlēṟṟi, ikaramum rakaramum varuvittu, vikārappaṭṭa uyirākiya ākārattiṉmuṉ uṭampaṭu mey yakāram varuvittu, ṟakara ukaraṅ keṭuttu, makara oṟṟu varuvittu muṭikka.			(58)
464.		āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kāli
					mutalī reṇṇi ṉukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu, avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu āyiram muṭiyu māṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āyirakkiḷavi varūuṅ kāli - āyirameṉṉuñ col oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varuṅkālattu, mutal īreṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum - oru iru eṉṉum iraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṭ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
ukaraṅ keṭu meṉavē, ēṉiyaṉa muṉṉark kūṟiyavāṟē niṟṟal peṟṟām.
utāraṇam: orāyiram, irāyiram eṉa varum.	(59)
465.	mutaṉili nīṭiṉu māṉa milli.
iḵtu, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉili nīṭiṉum māṉamilli - ammutalīreṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa okāra ikāraṅkaḷ nīṇṭumuṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ōrāyiram, īrāyiram, eṉa varum.	(60)
466.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.
itu, mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇ āyirattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vakāramākum - mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara oṟṟākat tirintu  muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: muvvāyiram eṉa varum.
muṉṉiṟ cūttirattu nili eṉṟataṉāṉ, itaṉi mutaṉili nīṭṭi vakara oṟṟuk keṭuttu mūvāyiram eṉavum              muṭikka.			(61)
467.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
itu, nāṉkeṉṉumeṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum - nāṉkeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟākat tirintu  muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nālāyiram eṉa varum.	(62)
468.	ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.
iḵtu - ainteṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē yakāramākum - ainteṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: aiyāyiram eṉa varum.	(63)
469.		āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiya lukara
					mīṟumey yoḻiyak keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu  āṟeṉṉu meṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram - āṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, mey oḻiya īṟukeṭutal vēṇṭum - atu tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟukkeṭātu niṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu tāṉ keṭṭup puṇartali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: aṟāyiram eṉa varum.
muṉṉar neṭumutal kuṟukum (eḻu. 440) eṉṟavaḻi aṟuveṉa niṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭuk kēṭu kūṟiṉā reṉpatu peṟṟām. eṉṉi? kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ ēṟimuṭitaliṉ. itu kuṟṟukaran tirintu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu muṭipu kūṟiṉār. muṟṟiyalukaram īṟumeyyoḻiyak keṭum eṉavē, kuṟṟukaraṅ keṭātu ēṟimuṭiyu meṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ peṟutum.
utāraṇam: 21āṟāyiram eṉa varum.
maruṅ keṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum neṭu mutal kuṟukātu niṉṟu muṭital koḷka. 	āṟākuvatē (col. 80) eṉa varum.	(64)
470.		oṉpā ṉiṟuti yuruvunili tiriyā
					tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi marapē.
iḵtu  oṉpateṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti - oṉpateṉṉu meṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaram, uruvunili tiriyātu - taṉ vaṭivunili tirintu keṭātē, cāriyi marapu iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - cāriyiyākiya marapiṉiyuṭiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: oṉpatiṉāyiram  eṉa varum.
uruveṉṟum nili yeṉṟuñ cāriyimarapu yeṉṟuṅ kūṟiya mikiyāṉ, āyiramallāta piṟaveṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭ peyariṭattum iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipu koḷka. oṉpatiṟṟuk kōṭi, oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu, oṉpatiṟṟut taṭakki  oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē, vēṟoru muṭipiṉmiyiṟ 22kūṟā toḻinta eṇṇāyira meṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, īṇṭuk kūṟiyavaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutaluṅ koḷka. aḷavāku moḻi mutal (eḻu. 121) eṉpataṉuḷ, niliiya eṉṟataṉāṉ 23ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.	(65)
471.		nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kāli
					nūṟa ṉiyaṟki mutaṉilik kiḷavi.
iḵtu - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉṉu meṇ aṭiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟāyiram muṉ varūuṅ kāli - nūṟāyiram eṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttamoḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, mutaṉilik kiḷavi nūṟaṉ iyaṟki - oṉṟeṉṉum mutaṉilikkiḷavi oṉṟumuṉ nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇoṭu muṭintāṟ pōla vikārameyti muṭiyum eṉavē vaḻinilik kiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutaliya eṇkaḷ vikārameytiyum, eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: oru nūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ēṉiyaṉa irunūṟāyiram iraṇṭunūṟāyiram, munnūṟāyiram mūṉṟu nūṟāyiram, nāṉūṟāyiram nāṉkunūṟāyiram, ainnūṟā yiram aintu nūṟāyiram aṟunūṟāyiram āṟunūṟāyiram, eṇṇū ṟāyiram eṭṭunūṟāyiram, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram  eṉa varum. ivvikārappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramākki ukaram varuvittu, mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukki, mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu nakāramākki, nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā vākki, eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakāra mākki, ilēcukaḷāṟ koṇṭa ceykikaḷil vēṇṭu vaṉavuṅ koṇarntu muṭikka.
ēṟpuḻikkōṭa leṉpataṉāṟ, 24toḷḷāyirameṉṟa muṭipi ṉoṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyā teṉṟu koḷka. muṉ eṉpataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi peṟṟu oṉpati ṉūṟāyiram eṉṟumām. nili eṉpataṉāṉ, mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāka muṭivuḻi neṭumutal kuṟukāmi koḷka.	(66)
472.		nūṟeṉ kiḷavi yoṉṟumuta leṉpāṟ
					kīṟuciṉi yoḻiya viṉavoṟṟu mikumē.
itu, nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi - nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇup peyar, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya - īṟākiya kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭātuniṟpa, iṉa oṟṟu mikum - acciṉikku iṉamākiya ṟakara oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa varum. iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu aḷavuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
25īṟuciṉi eṉṟu ōtiya mikiyāṉ, nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu piṟa eṇṇum, piṟaporuṭpeyarum, ivvitiyum, piṟavitiyum eyti muṭital koḷka. nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟut toṇṇūṟu eṉavum; nūṟṟukkuṟi, nūṟṟitaḻttāmari (aiṅkuṟu. 20), nūṟṟukkāṇam, nūṟṟukkāṉmaṇṭapam eṉavum iṉa oṟṟu mikkaṉa koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, irunūṟ ṟoṉṟu iraṇṭunūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa nūṟu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyuṅ   koḷka.		(67)
473.		aviyūr pattiṉu mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu - annūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ aṭiyaṭuttavaḻip puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avi ūrpattiṉum - annūṟu eṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷi ūrntu vanta patteṉpata ṉōṭu puṇaru miṭattum, attoḻiṟṟākum - īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: nūṟṟorupaḵtu; irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu, eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum. maṟṟu, nūṟṟoṉpatu avi ūrappaṭṭu vantatu aṉmi uṇarka. ākumeṉṟataṉāṉ, orunūṟṟorupaḵtu irunūṟ ṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipuṅ koḷka.		(68)
474.		aḷavu niṟiyu māyiya ṟiriyātu
					kuṟṟiya lukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyu
					muṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyātu - nūṟeṉpata ṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ tiriyātu iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyum - avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ keṭā miyum iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭarmoḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ varu moḻi valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum, muṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar - valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikumē (eḻut. 426) eṉa vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk kūṟiya taṉmiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	nūṟṟukkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
tiriyā teṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟeṉpatu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. aḵtu orunūṟṟukkalam irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa varum.			(69)
475.		oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
					yoṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē
					niṉṟa vāytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu  oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭum oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi - oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākap patteṉṉum eṇ ēṟi orucollāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ, oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarum iṭattu, niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum-paḵteṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭṭu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ, oṟṟu iṭimikum - āṇṭu iṉa oṟṟākiya oru takara oṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu, orupattiraṇṭu, irupat tiraṇṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ 26orupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇkaḷ atikārattāl niṉṟa nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttu varumeṉṟu uṇarka.		(70)
476.		āyiram variṉē yiṉṉeṉ cāriyi
					yāvayi ṉoṟṟiṭi mikuta lilli.
iḵtu  orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu āyirattip puṇark kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram variṉ iṉṉeṉ cāriyi - avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi āyirattōṭu puṇarum iṭattu iṉ cāriyi peṟum, āvayiṉ oṟṟu iṭi mikutal illi - av viṭattut takara oṟṟu iṭi vantu mikātu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: orupatiṉāyiram irupatiṉāyiram eṉa eṇpatiṉ kāṟum oṭṭuka. ivi nūṟṟorupatiṉāyiram eṉavum varum.
āvayiṉ eṉṟataṉāl, nūṟāyirattorupattīrāyiram eṉṟāṟ pōla attup peṟutalum piṟavuṅ koḷka.	(71)
477.	aḷavu niṟiyu māyiya ṟiriyā.
iḵtu - oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā - orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭimikātu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: orupatiṉkalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; orupatiṉkaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku nūṟu aṭi yaṭuttu oṭṭuka.
tiriyā eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam irupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṉṉiṉ akaram ṟakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭutalum, ukaramum valleḻuttup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṟavaḻi varumoḻi nakaran tirintuḻi nilimoḻi ṉakarakkēṭuṅ koḷka.
aḷavu niṟiyu mataṉō raṉṉa eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvār, muṉṉarc cūttirattu āvayiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉum, ataṉ muṉṉarc cūttirattu niṉṟa eṉṟataṉāṉum ivaṟṟi muṭippār.	(72)
478.		mutaṉili yeṇṇiṉ muṉ valleḻuttu variṉu
					ñanamat tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyiyiṉu
					mutaṉili yiyaṟki yeṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭ peyarip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ñanama tōṉṟiṉum - ña na ma kkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉum, yava vantu iyiyiṉum - ya va kkaḷākiya iṭiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, mutaṉili iyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvoṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉey tiya muṭipu nilimi eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, vaḻiniliyeṇṇākiya iraṇṭu mutalākiya eṇkaḷ ammūṉṟu kaṇamum mutaṉmoḻiyāyvariṉ, mutaṉili muṭipāki vikāram eytiyum, eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
utāraṇam: orukal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum; irukal; iraṇṭukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivveṇkaḷiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu, mutalīreṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāramāka ukaram vantatu. irukal mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku iṭinili rakāraṅ keṭukka. mukkal mūṉṟukal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. itaṟku neṭu mutal kuṟukki mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum (eḻut. 447) eṉpata ṉāṉ muṭikka. muṉṉar, eṇṇuppeyarum aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum varuvaḻikkūṟiya vikāraṅkaḷiṟ, poruṭ peyarkkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittu, ellā vaṟṟiṟkum nili yeṉṟata ṉāṉ, 27oṟṟuttirittu muṭikka. avi, mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum ñakaram varuvaḻi ñakara oṟṟātalum, mūṉṟaṟku yakaram varuvaḻi vakara oṟṟātalumām.
utāraṇam: nāṟkal nāṉkukal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi; nāṉñāṇ; nāṉkuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; aiṅkal aintukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi; aiññāṇ aintuñāṇ; nūl maṇi; aiyāḻ aintuyāḻ aivaṭṭu aintuvaṭṭu; aṟukal āṟukal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; eṇkal eṭṭukkal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi; eṇ ñāṇ eṭṭuñāṇ; nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; oṉpatu kal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉpatiṉkal eṉac ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻak kiṉmiyiṉ oḻikka. iṉṉum māṭṭēṟiṉṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām muṭipu nili yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka.	(73)
479.		ataṉili yuyirkkum yāvaru kāli
					mutaṉili yokara mōvā kummē
					rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.
iḵtu - oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu, poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutalmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum, mēṟkūṟiya yakāram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉilikku - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku, uyirum yāvum varukāli - uyir mutaṉ moḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ataṉ nili - ammutaṉiliyiṉ taṉmi ivvāṟām; okaram ōvākum - okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum; rakarattu ukaran tuvarak keṭum - rakarattu mēṉiṉṟa ukaram muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
nāṉkāvataṉi mutaṉiliyōṭu kūṭṭi, ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa ummiyi uyirōṭum yāvōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. eṉavē vaḻinili yeṇkaḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṟkūṟiyavāṟē iru vāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.
utāraṇam: 	ōraṭi, ōrākam, ōrili, ōrīṭṭam, ōruli, ōrūcal, ōreḻu, ōrēṭu, ōriyam, ōroḻuṅku, ōrōli ōruviyam eṉa varum. kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, mutaleṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramākkuka. ōryāḻ, ōryāṉi eṉa varum. tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ, iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ikaratti nīṭṭi, rakarattuḷ ukarattik keṭuttu, īraci īryāṉi eṉavum, mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaravoṟṟuk keṭuttu, mūvaci mūyāṉi eṉavum muṭikka. ivi ceyyuṇ muṭipu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa piṟavum aṟintu muṭittuk koḷka.	(74)
480.		iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
					vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
					makara vaḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.
iḵtu - iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya māveṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ  iṟuti muṉṉar - iraṇ ṭeṉṉu meṇ mutalāka oṉpateṉṉumeṇ īṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uriya mā veṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy variṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu - avveṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭi yeṉṉum aḷavup peyarōṭu ottu vikārappaṭṭu muṭitalum urittu; ummiyāṉ vikārap paṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
vaḻakkiyal vaḻaṅkiyaleṉa vikāram. makaraaḷavu makara mutaṉ moḻiyākiya aḷavup peyareṉap paṇputtoki. aḵtu aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum (eḻu. 446) eṉpataṉuḷ, oru maṇṭi eṉa muṭittatām.
utāraṇam: irumā, mummā, nāṉmā, aimmā, aṟumā, eṇmā, oṉpatiṉmā eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiya cūttiraṅ kaḷāṉ vikārap paṭutti muṭikka. iṉi, ummiyāṉ vikārappaṭuttātu iraṇṭumā mūṉṟumā nāṉkumā aintumā āṟumā eṭṭumā oṉpatumā eṉavum muṭikka. puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ aḷavu niṟiyum (eḻu. 389) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ, ēḻneṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. ataṉāṉ, īṇṭu eḻumā eṉa muṭikka. ēḻmāveṉa muṭital vaḻakkiṉṟu.
iraṇṭumutal oṉpā ṉeṉṟu eṭuttōtiṉamiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumā veṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi, oṉṟumā veṉṉum muṭipu illi yāyiṟṟu.
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ mā eṉṟār, vilaṅku māvi nīkkutaṟku.	(75)
481.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
					ṉummuṅ keḻuvu muḷappaṭap piṟavu
					maṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic
					ceyyuṭ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum
					vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉ - lakāra ṉakāra meṉṟu kūṟa varukiṉṟa puḷḷi īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭa - ummeṉṉuñ cāriyiyum keḻuveṉṉum cāriyiyum uṭpaṭa, piṟavum aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟi - piṟacāriyiyum appeṟṟip paṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ mey peṟa niliyum - ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷit toṭarpu paṭuttik kūṟumiṭattup poruḷpaṭa niṟkum, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷ vayiṉāṉa - vēṟṟumiyik kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum pōlum. itaṟku ummeṉṉuñ cāriyiyiṉ makaratti ammiṉiṟuti (eḻu. 129) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa cāriyiyun tiriyumeṉa nakara oṟṟākki nilimoḻi lakara oṟṟiṉmēl uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. villun tiṅkaḷum pōlu meṉpataṟku, villiṭit tiṅkaḷ pōlumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittup poruḷurikka. kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē (kuṟun. 71); itaṟkuk kallik keḻīiyiṉa eṉa urikka.                   mā nitikkiḻavaṉum pōṉm (akam. 66); itaṟkuk kiḻavaṉip pōṉmeṉa urikka. ivvummi ciṟappaṉṟu. kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ (akam. 98) itaṟkuk kāṉikkeḻīiya eṉa urikka.
iṉi aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyiṭi eṉṟataṉāṟ, keḻu eṉṟatu piṟa colliṭattē paṇikeḻu peruntiṟaṟ palvēṉ maṉṉar (maturik. 234), tuṟikeḻu mānti (naṟ. 35) eṉa iyal pāka varuvaṉavum; 28vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar (akam. 17) payaṅkeḻu mā maḻi (puṟam. 266) eṉa nilimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttut tiriya varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. 
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iccāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyuṅ koḷka. pūkkēḻ toṭali nuṭaṅka veḻuntu (akam. 28) tuṟi kēḻūraṉ koṭumi nāṇi (aiṅkuṟu. 11) ivaṟṟiṟku, iraṇṭāvatum ēṟpuḻi mūṉṟāvatum virikka. ceṅ kēḻ meṉkoṭi (akam. 80) eṉpuḻik keḻu veṉṉum uric col eḻuttup pirinticittal (col. 395) eṉpataṉāṉ nīṇṭateṉṟu uṇarka. mey yeṉṟataṉāṟ, pūkkēḻeṉ
(naṟ. 10) puḻi valloṟṟu mikutal koḷka. 
iṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṭ piṟacāriyi peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittuk koḷka.29	(76)
482.		uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yākik
					kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉu miciyiṉun tōṉṟi
					neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccoṟ kiḷaviyu
					muyartiṇi yaḵṟiṇi yāyiru maruṅki
					ṉimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyuñ
					ceyyuñ ceyta veṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
					meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiṟoku moḻiyum
					tammiyal kiḷappiṟ ṟammuṟ ṟāmvarūu
					meṇṇiṉ ṟokuti yuḷappaṭap piṟavu
					maṉṉavi yellā maruviṉ pāttiya
					puṇariya ṉiliyiṭi yuṇarat tōṉṟā.
iḵtu - ivvatikārattāṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivi yeṉa avaṟṟi eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki - kūṟuṅkāl uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāka niṟkum collāki, kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉum tōṉṟi - kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum ici yiṉāṉum piṟantu, neṟippaṭavārāk kuṟic coṟkiḷaviyum - oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmi kuṟinta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi āyiru maruṅkiṉ - uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyeṉṉum avviraṇṭiṭattum uḷa vākiya, aimpālaṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum - oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu pala eṉṉum aintu pāliṉiyum aṟitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya 30paṇpu koḷpeyar tokun  tokiccollum, ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ - ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷi ṉuṭiya, mey oruṅku iyalun toḻil tokumoḻiyum - kālaṅkāṭṭum ummum akaramum oru coṟ kaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭipeyarcci tokku niṟkuñ coṟ kaḷum, tam iyal kiḷappiṉ-tamatu taṉmi kūṟumiṭattu, tammuṉ tāmvarūum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa - niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉart tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇup peyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa, aṉṉa piṟavum ellām - attaṉmiyākiya piṟavumellām, maruviṉ pāttiya -  ulakattu maruvi naṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyit tam ilakkaṇamāka vuṭiya, puṇariyal nili iṭi yuṇarat tōṉṟā - oṉṟaṉō ṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmi iṭam viḷaṅkat              tōṉṟā eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	kaṇ viṇṇa viṇittatu, viṇviṇittatu ivi kuṟippuriccol; āṭi veḷḷaviḷarttatu, veḷviḷarttatu ivi paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla volittatu, ollolittatu ivi iciyuric col. ollolinīr pāyvatē pōlun tuṟivaṉ eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum. ivi, uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakka lākāmiyiṉ neṟippaṭavārā eṉṟār. viṇṇa viṇittatu teṟipputtōṉṟat teṟittateṉṟum, viṇ viṇittatu teṟipput teṟittateṉṟum ām. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṉ taṉmi kuṟinta collā yiṟṟu. viṉiyē kuṟippē (col. 258) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya eṉa eṉpataṉi ivaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭic collātaliṉ, viṇṇeṉa viṇittatu eṉap puṇarkkappaṭu māṟu uṇarka.
iṉik karumpārppāṉ, karumpārppaṉi, karumpārppār, karuṅ kutiri, karuṅkutirikaḷ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ, kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi, kariyarākiya pārppār, kariyatākiya kutiri, kariyaṉavākiya kutirikaḷ eṉa aimpāli ṉiyum uṇarttum paṇpu koḷpeyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuṭ karumi eṉṉum paṇpup peyar tokkatēṟ karumi yākiya pārppāṉeṉa virittal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam viriyā miyiṟ paṇpukoḷpeyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka.
veṟṟili, veṟṟuppili, veṟṟaṭi, veṟṟeṉat toṭuttal eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, veṟuvitākiyili yeṉpatu, pākkuṅ 31kōṭṭunūṟuṅ kūṭātatāya paṇpuṇarttiya īṟu tokutaliṉ maruviṉ pāttiyatāy niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu. veṟuvitākiya uppili yeṉṟatu, ciṟitum uppiliyeṉa niṉṟatu. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa. iṅṅaṉam aimpālun tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉili yātaliṟ puṇarttalākāmi kūṟiṉār.
iṉi, āṭaraṅku, ceykuṉṟu, puṇarpoḻutu, arivāḷ, kol yāṉi, celcelavu eṉa, nilam mutalākiya peyareccan tokka viṉit tokikaḷi virikkuṅkāl, āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉac ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum, āṭā niṉṟavaraṅku, āṭumaraṅku eṉac ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum, avaṟṟāṉāya puṭi peyarcciyit tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum oru coṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkkalākāmiyiṟ, puṇarkkalākāteṉṟār. ummiṟuti nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttumāṟu, viṉiyiṉ tokuti (col. 415) eṉṉum eccaviyaṟ cūttirattuṭ kūṟutum. iv um īṟu iraṇṭu kālamum oruṅkuṇarttutaṟ ciṟappu nōkkic ceyta eṉpataṉi āciriyar muṟkūṟārāyiṉar.
iṉip pattu eṉa niṟuttip, patteṉat tantu puṇarkkap paṭātu, pappatteṉavum, paḵpatteṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. orōvoṉ ṟeṉpatum atu. atutāṉē ōroṉṟōroṉ ṟākak koṭu eṉṟāṟ puṇarkkappaṭum.
iṉi, aṉṉapiṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉē, uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi uṇ eṉṉum mutaṉiliyuṅ, kālaṅkāṭṭum ṭakaramum, iṭaṉum pālum uṇarttum āṉum oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarkkappaṭā, avi niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum aṉmiyiṉ. kariyaṉ eṉpuḻik, karu eṉa niṟutti aṉ eṉat tantu puṇarkkap paṭātu, aḵtu iṉṉa ṉeṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ. ēṉi viṉiccoṟkaḷum ivvāṟē pirittup puṇarkka lākāmi uṇarka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻik koḷ eṉpataṉi eṉa eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarkkap paṭāmiyum, ūraṉ veṟpaṉ mutaliya viṉip peyarkaḷum, piṟavum puṇarkkappaṭāmiyum koḷka. ivvāciriyar puṇarkkappaṭā veṉṟa ic coṟkaḷiyum, vaṭanūṟkaṇ muṭitta aṉakaṉ aṉapāyaṉ akaḷaṅkaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum 32piṉṉuḷḷōr muṭittal mutaṉūloṭu māṟukoḷak kūṟalā meṉṟu uṇarka.	(77)
483.		kiḷanta valla ceyyuḷuṭ ṭirinavum
					vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
					viḷampiya viyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
					vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉa roḻukka
					ṉaṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu - ivvatikārattu eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟi yellām itaṉāṉē muṭikka eṉa atikārap puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum - muṉṉar eṭuttōtappaṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu muṭivaṉavum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum-nālvaki vaḻakkum naṭakku miṭattu maruvutalōṭu tirintu muṭivaṉavum, viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ-muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇa muṟimi yiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟumāyiṉ avaṟṟi, naṉmati nāṭṭattu-nalla aṟiviṉatu ārāycciyāṉē, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ-vaḻakku muṭintu naṭakku miṭattē, uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal eṉmaṉār pulavar-muṭipu vēṟupāṭukaḷi aṟintu naṭāttuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	taṭavuttiri eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum taṭavunili (puṟam. 140) eṉa ukaram peṟṟum akara īṟṟu uriccol vantatu. atavattaṅkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ akara īṟu attup peṟṟatu. 33kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ eṉa akara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉart takaraṅ koṭukka.
34naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi naṟpōrc cempiyaṉ
	kurava nīṭiya koṉṟiyaṅ kāṉal
eṉa ākāra iṟuti kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭṭu iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa.
muḷavumāt tolicciya pinniṇap piḷavi
piṇavunāy muṭukkiya taṭiyoṭu virii	(malipaṭu - 176)
eṉa avvīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟāta muṭipupeṟṟaṉa. tiṇvār vicitta muḻavo ṭākuḷi (malipaṭu. 3), cuṟaveṟi mīṉ, iravaḻaṅku ciṟuneṟi (akam. 318) ivi ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa. kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkali (akam. 97) eṉa ikara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa. tīyiṉaṉṉa voṇ ceṅ kāntaḷ (malipaṭu. 145) eṉa īkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉ peṟṟatu. nalloḻukkaṅ kākkun tiruvattavar (nālaṭi. 457) eṉa ukara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attuppeṟṟatu. ēppeṟṟa māṉpiṇi pōl (cīvaka. 2945) eṉa ēkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu. kit tuṇṭām pōḻtē (nālaṭi. 19), kittillār nallar (nāṉmaṇik. 69) eṉavum, puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉal (akam. 80), mulliyan toṭiyal eṉavum, aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. aṇṇal kōyil vaṇṇamē (cīvaka. 126) eṉa ōkāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.
iṉi 35añcevi niṟiya vāliṉa (mullip. 89) eṉa al vaḻikkaṭ kakaramum akaramuṅkeṭṭaṉa. maravam pāvi vayiṟārap paruki maravanākam vaṇaṅki māṟkaṇam eṉa iruvaḻiyum makaram vikārappaṭṭu ammup peṟṟaṉa. kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉēmāka (puṟam. 144), poṉṉan tikiri muṉcamat turuṭṭi (puṟam. 368) poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭṭiṟ poliveyti eṉa ṉakara īṟu iruvaḻiyum ammuppeṟṟaṉa. vērpiṇi vetirattuk kālporu naralici (naṟ. 62) eṉa rakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu. nāvalan taṇpoḻil (perumpāṇ. 465), kāṉalam peruntuṟi (aiṅkuṟu. 158) eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa. neytalañ ciṟupaṟi iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. āyiṭi yirupē rāṇmi ceyta pūcal (kuṟun. 43) eṉpuḻi āyiṭi yeṉpatu urupātaliṉ nīṭavarutal (eḻut. 208) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. temmuṉi eṉat tevveṉpuḻi vakaraṅ keṭṭu makara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintatu. a eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu aṉṟiyaṉittum eṉat tirintatu. mutirkōṅkiṉ muki (kuṟiñcikkali. 20) eṉavum, kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻam (cīvaka. 31) eṉavum, kuṟṟukara īṟu iṉ peṟutaluṅ koḷka. 36toṇṭu taliyiṭṭa pattukkuṟi (tol. poruḷ. 413) eṉa ṇakaram iraṭṭātu takara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟāyk kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟi muṭintatu.
iṅṅaṉañ ceyyuḷuṭ piṟavum tirivaṉa uḷavēṉum ip puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka. arumaruntāṉeṉpatu - rakaravukaraṅ keṭṭu arumantāṉeṉa muṭintatu. cōḻaṉāṭu - cōṇāṭu eṉa aṉ keṭṭu muṭintatu. pāṇṭi nāṭum atu. toṇṭi mānāṭu  - toṇṭi nāṭeṉa īṟṟeḻuttuc cila keṭṭu muṭintatu. maliyamāṉāṭu - malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻut toḻintaṉa palavuṅ keṭṭu muṭintatu. potuvil eṉpatu - potiyileṉa ukaran tirintu ikaramāy yakara uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟu muṭintatu. piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ellām ippuṟaṉaṭiyāṉ amittuk koḷka.	(78)
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	itaṉi ēḻeṉṟu koḷvatu acipaṟṟi. acikaḷāvaṉa nēraci nāṉkum niriyaci nāṉkum. aviyāvaṉa:-
	1. atu - kuṟil taṉiyēvanta nēraci. 2. kokku - kuṟil oṟṟōṭu vanta nēraci. 3. kāṭu - neṭil taṉiyē vanta nēraci. 4. pāṭṭu - neṭil oṟṟōṭu vanta nēraci. 5. marapu - kuṟiliṇi taṉiyē vanta niriyaci. 6. varampu - kuṟiliṇi oṟṟōṭu vanta niriyaci. 7. palācu - kuṟil neṭil iṇinta niriyaci. 8. karāmpu - kuṟil neṭil oṟṟōṭu vanta niriyaci eṉpaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉvanta ukaram muṟṟiyalukaramākaliṉ ataṉi nīkki ēṉiya ēḻacippiṉṉum vantaṉa kuṟṟiyalukaramātal kāṇka. itaṉi `neṭilē kuṟiliṇi kuṟiṉeṭi leṉṟivi - oṟṟoṭu varutaloṭu kuṟṟoṟ ṟiṟutiyeṉ - ṟēḻkuṟṟukarak kiṭaṉeṉa moḻipa' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉaṟika.
	iṅṅaṉam ēḻacipaṟṟik kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ koḷvārkkup piṇṇākku. cuṇṇāmpu, paṭṭāṅku mutaliyaṉavum, varuvatu pōvatu mutaliya ṉavum ēḻaciyuḷaṭaṅkāvātaliṉ acikoḷḷappaṭāveṉa maṟuppar naṉṉul viruttiyurikārarum. (naṉṉūl - cū. 94.)
2.	niṟiyum eṉavum pāṭam.
